# S8.7 — Plans per storey and the stepped band Implementation Plan

> **Status:** Done (controller, D-098; pending the owner's reading) · **Date:** 2026-10-02 · **Roadmap:** [S8.7](2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) (section S8, Typologies) · **Decisions:** D-009, D-026, D-031, D-046, D-061, D-062, D-063, D-064, D-065, D-068, D-071, D-074, D-075, D-079, D-085, D-087, D-090, D-091, D-093, D-095, D-097, D-098, D-099, D-105; ADR-015 (plans per storey, created in Task 2, so not linked here), [ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md) (the specification of `SYN-TYP-012`, Task 7), [ADR-013](../decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md) (storeys that differ, Task 7), [ADR-011](../decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md), [ADR-008](../decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)
>
> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implement S8.7 of D-098: plans that differ by storey (D-095), decided in a new ADR-015, and the last family of the four-source set, `SYN-TYP-012` (*Stepped Band*, stepped dwelling bands with exposed terraces), as ADR-006 specifies it, with its Grasshopper component and icon. A characterisation test first confirms that every floor aggregator already designates the part of a lower storey's ceiling that the storey above leaves free as an outdoor roof (a terrace) and the covered part as lying between the storeys (D-068), with ground and roof areas that validate, so no aggregation or validation rule changes. A new base class `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` returns one ordinary plan per storey, bottom-up, sharing every rule of `PlanGenerator` through one internal helper, so the sixteen existing generators keep their outputs. `SteppedBandGenerator` is its first subclass: a west stair, a row of dwellings, and a rear corridor that every dwelling adjoins, stepping back `StepDepth` per storey. The band validates with every simplifier and aggregator on its canonical plans, rotated and moved by two placements, with 2 and 4 storeys, and with 4 at the second placement. In Grasshopper the generator's *Plan* output is a list of the storeys, wired item-wise through a simplifier into an aggregator. Every existing plan, floor, building, and snapshot stays unchanged. S8.7 ends with version 0.8.6 and the tag `v0.8.6`, and with it S8 is complete: 18 plan generators. After the stage gate and a fresh clone of the tag pass `scripts/verify.ps1`, the controller pushes `main` and `v0.8.6` (D-098); S6 (Convert2IDF) follows.

**Architecture:** `Lod.Core.Plans` gains `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>`, a sibling of `PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` with the same constructors, `Name`, `Validate`, `Describe`, and `IsPositive`, whose `Layout` returns one `PlanLayout` per storey and whose `Generate` returns `Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>`. The checks of the common parameters and presets and the build of one plan (zones, surfaces, centred windows within `GeometryLimits`, provenance) move from `PlanGenerator` into the internal static class `PlanBuilder`, which both bases call, so `PlanGenerator`'s behaviour, diagnostics, and provenance are identical. A multi-storey plan records `Storey` (from 0) in its provenance, and a storey's diagnostics are prefixed `Storey {k}: `. The family is a folder `Lod.Generators/SteppedBand/` with a parameter record (a static `Default` holding the ADR-006 defaults), a preset record with one slot per space type, and the generator. No floor aggregator, simplifier, validation rule, or pipeline type changes: the storeys are floor entries with multiplier 1. `TypologyGeneratorComponent` gains a list output for multi-storey families, whose storeys are previewed at their elevations; one component derives from it, and `scripts/make_icons.py` draws one new icon.

**Tech Stack:** C# 12 (`LangVersion` 12.0); .NET SDK ≥ 8 (`global.json` pins 8.0.100 with `rollForward: latestMajor`); `netstandard2.0` core libraries; Clipper2 2.0.0; RhinoCommon and Grasshopper 8.19.25132.1001 (plugin targets `net7.0`); xUnit 2.9.3, xunit.runner.visualstudio 3.1.5, Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk 17.14.1 (tests target `net8.0`); Windows PowerShell 5.1 for `scripts/verify.ps1`; Python 3.10 or later with Pillow for `scripts/make_icons.py` (the icons in this plan were drawn with Pillow 12.3.0).

## Global Constraints

- **Gate:** S8.6 is complete on `main` (`a4db33e`, tag `v0.8.5`, D-105), with D-104 and D-105 recorded; this plan is committed to `main` before Task 1 ("docs(plans): add the s8.7 per-storey plans and stepped band plan"); it is the only file of that commit. ADR-006 (with the S8.4 specification of `SYN-TYP-012`, its defaults, zone IDs, order, and validation), ADR-011, ADR-013, and ADR-014 are accepted; D-095 asks for the ADR on plans per storey that Task 2 adds. Before Task 1 the working tree is clean (`git status --short` prints nothing).
- Rhino 8 (8.19 or later) only (D-003); stages exchange Grasshopper objects, not JSON (D-017).
- `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` have no RhinoCommon or Grasshopper reference; their tests run with plain `dotnet test`.
- Nullable reference types on; warnings are errors; `.editorconfig` rules are enforced in the build; every public member of `Lod.Core` and `Lod.Generators` has XML docs (CS1591 is an error).
- Generation is deterministic; outputs have a stable order: a multi-storey generator returns its storeys bottom-up, from storey 0; the stepped band lists on every storey `ST`, `CO`, then `U1…Un` west to east.
- **Counts.** The stepped band needs at least 2 storeys (one step) and has no upper bound (ADR-006). A count below its minimum and every length that is not positive and finite are the error `InvalidParameter`, every error reported at once; when every length is positive, a `Length` not greater than `StairWidth` and a `BaseDepth` that the steps use up on the top storey are errors too, the latter only when the count is valid.
- Numerical tolerances come only from `ToleranceSettings` (ADR-004); S8.7 adds none and changes none, and `ToleranceSettings.cs` is not touched. The minimum wall and window geometry stays in `GeometryLimits` (ADR-011), unchanged.
- **Existing outputs do not change.** The twenty-four existing snapshots (`linear-plan-canonical`, `stack-two-storeys`, the three `simplifier-*`, the three `aggregator-*`, the S8.1 snapshots `stair-bay-bar-canonical`, `stair-pair-canonical`, `gallery-bar-canonical`, `terrace-row-canonical`, the S8.2 snapshots `point-plate-canonical`, `winged-band-canonical`, `open-court-canonical`, the S8.3 snapshot `enclosed-court-canonical`, the S8.4 snapshots `office-plate-canonical` and `cafeteria-canonical`, the S8.5 snapshots `radial-lobes-canonical`, `branching-mall-canonical`, `care-hub-canonical`, and `foyer-halls-canonical`, and the S8.6 snapshots `operating-suite-canonical` and `court-cluster-canonical`) stay byte-identical, and every existing test keeps passing; no test is removed, and no existing icon PNG changes. The move of `PlanGenerator`'s body into `PlanBuilder` (Task 3) changes no output: every existing generator test and snapshot is run against it.
- **Never weaken a check (AGENTS.md rules 11, 12).** Tasks 3 and 4 start from tests that fail to build, the way each task says. The characterisation tests of Task 1 and the integration matrix of Task 5 pass at once, because they confirm behaviour that already exists; each task says to stop if a case fails, since a pipeline change needs its own test-first task. No tolerance is widened, no validation check is relaxed, and no test is weakened or removed.
- **Report, never repair (GLOBAL.md quality rule 6).** A storey that *Perimeter Core* cannot zone fails with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087), never repaired; the generator does not impose that precondition, and Task 5 tests it on a 7 m top storey. Zones about 1 m wide on rotated plans remain the known limit of D-090; S8.7 does not change it.
- **Typed records per family** (AGENTS.md): the stepped band has its own parameter and preset records; there is no catch-all parameter class and no dictionary of parameters. The presets are `DwellingUnit`, `Corridor`, and `Stair`, each checked against its slot's space type once for all storeys (`PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`). No space type and no preset is added.
- **Housing zoning (D-009, D-085).** One zone per dwelling and storey; the stair and the corridor are one zone each per storey.
- **Source contract (D-093).** ADR-006 and `docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md` hold everything this plan needs; `typology-synthesizer` is not opened while this plan is executed.
- **No backward compatibility (D-064).** One component is added with a new GUID: *Stepped Band* `3c685bfb-b45b-45af-98d2-8318a6314922`. No existing component, input, output, or GUID changes; the other typology components keep their single *Plan* output.
- Grasshopper components are thin adaptors (AGENTS.md): every rule lives in `Lod.Core` or `Lod.Generators`; a component reads inputs, calls the generator, and reports its diagnostics.
- Research equivalence rules are not changed (GLOBAL.md scientific rule 7): S8.7 adds a generator base and a generator and changes no step of the pipeline, so the research documentation needs no edit; ADR-015 and ADR-013 record that storeys that differ need no new aggregation rule.
- **Links.** No commit contains a relative link to a file that a later commit creates: ADR-015 (Task 2) is committed before any document links to it.
- **Code fences.** Every Markdown file this plan touches keeps balanced code fences: every opening fence line carries a language, every closing fence line is bare, and no file ends inside a block. Text this plan appends to a file is the content of a code block, never the fence lines around it; Tasks 7 and 9 run a check that prints each file's state.
- No simulation inside the pipeline (D-016). No CI (D-005): `scripts/verify.ps1` passes locally before every merge to `main`.
- Commits: `type(scope): summary` in lower case, no agent, model, or tool attribution and no `Co-Authored-By` trailers (D-001, D-002); author identity per D-050.
- **Pushes (D-098).** Slices are rebased onto local `main` and nothing is pushed while the stage runs. After the close-out (Task 9), its tag, and a fresh clone of `v0.8.6` that passes `scripts/verify.ps1`, the controller pushes `main` and `v0.8.6` (Task 10); never a force push.
- `.gh` example definitions are made and saved by a person in Rhino 8; agents never write or check them (D-058). S8.7 adds none.
- S8.7 changes these earlier files: `PlanGenerator.cs` (S2, last changed in S8.1), `TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (S8.1, last changed in S8.6), `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1, last changed in S8.6), and `Directory.Build.props` (S0, last changed in S8.6); and the documents AGENTS.md, README.md, ADR-006, `pipeline.md`, `program-presets.md`, `syn-typologies.md`, the roadmap, `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md`, and the decision log (all last changed in S8.6), ADR-013 (last changed in S8.3), and `domain-model.md` (last changed in S8.4).

## Slice A — `feature/per-storey-plans`

Plans per storey: the characterisation of the terraces first (it passes at once, which is the finding ADR-015 rests on), then ADR-015, then the base class, test-first.

### Task 1: Terraces of storeys that step back, with every floor aggregator

**Files:**
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Core.Tests/Buildings/TerraceTests.cs` (the folder is new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (S2–S8.6): `PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>`, `PlanParameters`, `PlanPresets.Slots`, `PresetSlot`, `PlanLayout`, `PlannedZone`, `Polygon2.Rectangle`, `NoSimplification`, `FloorEntry`, the four aggregators `Stack`, `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, and `SingleZoneBuilding` (ADR-013) with the designation of floors and ceilings on the assembled stack (D-068) and the warning `UnsupportedStorey` (D-074), `HorizontalSurface`, `HorizontalKind`, `BoundaryCondition`, `IGeneratedBuilding.InternalMasses` (D-031), `Totals.Of` (`GroundArea`, `RoofArea`, `ExposedFloorArea`), `BuildingValidator`, `ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit` and `Corridor`.
- Produces: 11 tests on a private test generator of one storey, a dwelling `U1` over `[0, 10] × [South, 8]` and a corridor `C1` over `[0, 10] × [8, 10]`, run for South 0, 3, and 6 m (100, 70, and 40 m²) and stacked bottom-up. With every aggregator and multiplier 1: each lower storey's ceiling keeps a 30 m² piece facing outdoors (the terrace) and the covered part lies between the storeys (`Interzone`, `Adiabatic` in `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier`, or internal mass in `SingleZoneBuilding`); the top roof is 40 m²; no floor is exposed and none is unsupported; ground and roof area are 100 m² each and the building validates, with the checks `Building.GroundArea`, `Building.RoofArea`, and `Building.ExposedFloorArea` enforced and passed (4 tests). `Stack` pairs `L0/U1` with `L1/U1` over 50 m² and `L0/C1` with `L1/C1` over 20 m², with the 30 m² terrace outdoors; `SingleZonePerFloorType` gives `E0`, `E1`, `E2` with interzone pairs over the covered parts and no internal mass; `SingleZoneBuilding` turns only the covered parts into internal mass (3 tests). Multipliers 2, 1, 2 also validate with every aggregator, the exposure never multiplied (D-046) (4 tests). No production code changes.

- [x] **Step 1: Create the branch**

```bash
git switch -c feature/per-storey-plans main
```

- [x] **Step 2: Write the characterisation tests — create `tests/Lod.Core.Tests/Buildings/TerraceTests.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Buildings;

/// <summary>
/// Storeys that step back (D-095, ADR-015): every floor aggregator designates the part of a lower storey's ceiling that the storey above does not
/// cover as an outdoor roof (a terrace) and the covered part as lying between the two storeys (D-068), with ground and roof areas that validate.
/// </summary>
public sealed class TerraceTests
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Three storeys 10 m long: a dwelling <c>U1</c> from <c>y = South</c> to 8 m and a corridor <c>C1</c> from 8 m to 10 m, with South 0, 3, and 6 m,
    /// so storey 0 is 100 m², storey 1 70 m², storey 2 40 m², and each lower storey keeps a 10 m × 3 m terrace.
    /// </summary>
    private static readonly double[] Souths = { 0.0, 3.0, 6.0 };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Aggregators() =>
        new[] { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" }.Select(a => new object[] { a });

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Aggregators))]
    public void TheUncoveredPartOfALowerCeilingIsAnOutdoorRoofAndTheCoveredPartLiesBetweenStoreys(string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> result = Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(Floors().Select(f => new FloorEntry(f, 1)).ToArray());

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey);
        IGeneratedBuilding building = result.Value;
        HorizontalSurface[] horizontal = building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(30.0, Outdoor(horizontal, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, 3.0), 9);
        Assert.Equal(30.0, Outdoor(horizontal, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, 6.0), 9);
        Assert.Equal(40.0, Outdoor(horizontal, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, 9.0), 9);
        Assert.Equal(70.0, Covered(building, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, 3.0), 9);
        Assert.Equal(40.0, Covered(building, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, 6.0), 9);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, Outdoor(horizontal, HorizontalKind.Floor, 3.0) + Outdoor(horizontal, HorizontalKind.Floor, 6.0), 9);
        Totals totals = Totals.Of(building.Zones, building.Surfaces, building.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(100.0, totals.GroundArea, 9);
        Assert.Equal(100.0, totals.RoofArea, 9);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, totals.ExposedFloorArea, 9);
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Validate(building);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.All(report.Checks.Where(c => c.Check is "Building.GroundArea" or "Building.RoofArea" or "Building.ExposedFloorArea"), c => Assert.True(c.Passed && c.Enforced, c.Check));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StackPairsTheCoveredPiecesWithTheZonesAboveAndLeavesTheTerraceOutdoors()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new Stack(ToleranceSettings.Default).Aggregate(Floors().Select(f => new FloorEntry(f, 1)).ToArray()).Value;

        HorizontalSurface[] ceilings = building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling && h.Zone.Value.StartsWith("L0/")).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("L0/U1", "L1/U1", 50.0), ("L0/U1", "-", 30.0), ("L0/C1", "L1/C1", 20.0) },
            ceilings.Select(h => (h.Zone.Value, h.AdjacentZone?.Value ?? "-", System.Math.Round(h.Area, 9))));
        Assert.Equal(BoundaryCondition.Outdoors, ceilings.Single(h => h.AdjacentZone is null).Boundary);
        Assert.Equal(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 0.0, 10.0, 3.0).Area, ceilings.Single(h => h.AdjacentZone is null).Polygon.Area, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorTypeGivesEveryStoreyItsZoneWithInterzonePairsOverTheCoveredParts()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZonePerFloorType(ToleranceSettings.Default).Aggregate(Floors().Select(f => new FloorEntry(f, 1)).ToArray()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "E0", "E1", "E2" }, building.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Empty(building.InternalMasses);
        HorizontalSurface[] interzone = building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(70.0, interzone.Where(h => h.Zone.Value == "E0" && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling && h.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value == "E1").Sum(h => h.Area), 9);
        Assert.Equal(70.0, interzone.Where(h => h.Zone.Value == "E1" && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Floor && h.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value == "E0").Sum(h => h.Area), 9);
        Assert.Equal(40.0, interzone.Where(h => h.Zone.Value == "E1" && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling && h.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value == "E2").Sum(h => h.Area), 9);
        Assert.Equal(40.0, interzone.Where(h => h.Zone.Value == "E2" && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Floor && h.AdjacentZone!.Value.Value == "E1").Sum(h => h.Area), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingTurnsOnlyTheCoveredPartsIntoInternalMass()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = new SingleZoneBuilding(ToleranceSettings.Default).Aggregate(Floors().Select(f => new FloorEntry(f, 1)).ToArray()).Value;

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(building.Zones);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 100.0, 70.0, 40.0 }, zone.Parts.Select(p => System.Math.Round(p.Footprint.Area, 9)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { (3.0, 70.0), (6.0, 40.0) }, building.InternalMasses.Select(m => (m.Elevation, System.Math.Round(m.SlabArea, 9))));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Surfaces, s => s.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Aggregators))]
    public void StoreysThatDifferMayStillStandForSeveralStoreysEach(string aggregator)
    {
        // Storey 0 x2, storey 1 x1, storey 2 x2: the band steps back above the second and the third storey only. Exposure is never multiplied (D-046).
        IFloor[] floors = Floors();
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floors[0], 2), new(floors[1], 1), new(floors[2], 2) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> result = Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(result.Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.UnsupportedStorey);
        IGeneratedBuilding building = result.Value;
        Totals totals = Totals.Of(building.Zones, building.Surfaces, building.OrientationDegrees);
        Assert.Equal(100.0, totals.GroundArea, 9);
        Assert.Equal(100.0, totals.RoofArea, 9);
        HorizontalSurface[] horizontal = building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(30.0, Outdoor(horizontal, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, 6.0), 9);
        Assert.Equal(30.0, Outdoor(horizontal, HorizontalKind.Ceiling, 9.0), 9);
        Dictionary<ZoneId, int> multiplier = building.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id, z => z.Multiplier);
        Assert.All(horizontal.Where(h => h.Boundary is BoundaryCondition.Outdoors or BoundaryCondition.Ground), h => Assert.Equal(1, multiplier[h.Zone]));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Validate(building);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    private static double Outdoor(IEnumerable<HorizontalSurface> horizontal, HorizontalKind kind, double elevation) =>
        horizontal.Where(h => h.Kind == kind && h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && System.Math.Abs(h.Elevation - elevation) < 1e-9).Sum(h => h.Area);

    /// <summary>The covered part of a slab: pieces between two storeys (interzone, or adiabatic in the zone multiplier) and internal mass.</summary>
    private static double Covered(IGeneratedBuilding building, HorizontalKind kind, double elevation) =>
        building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>()
            .Where(h => h.Kind == kind && h.Boundary is BoundaryCondition.Interzone or BoundaryCondition.Adiabatic && System.Math.Abs(h.Elevation - elevation) < 1e-9)
            .Sum(h => h.Area)
        + building.InternalMasses.Where(m => System.Math.Abs(m.Elevation - elevation) < 1e-9).Sum(m => m.SlabArea);

    private static IFloorAggregator Aggregator(string name) => name switch
    {
        "Stack" => new Stack(ToleranceSettings.Default),
        "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier" => new StackedFloorZoneMultiplier(ToleranceSettings.Default),
        "SingleZonePerFloorType" => new SingleZonePerFloorType(ToleranceSettings.Default),
        _ => new SingleZoneBuilding(ToleranceSettings.Default),
    };

    private static IFloor[] Floors()
    {
        var generator = new StepGenerator();
        var presets = new StepPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);
        return Souths.Select(south => new NoSimplification().Simplify(generator.Generate(new StepParameters(south), presets).Value).Value).ToArray();
    }

    private sealed record StepParameters(double South)
        : PlanParameters(3.0, 0.0);

    private sealed record StepPresets(ProgramPreset Unit, ProgramPreset Hall) : PlanPresets
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
        {
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Unit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Unit),
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Hall), SpaceType.Corridor, Hall),
        };
    }

    /// <summary>One storey of the stepped test band: <c>U1</c> over <c>[0, 10] × [South, 8]</c>, <c>C1</c> over <c>[0, 10] × [8, 10]</c>.</summary>
    private sealed class StepGenerator : PlanGenerator<StepParameters, StepPresets>
    {
        public StepGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public override string Name => nameof(StepGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(StepParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<Diagnostic>();

        protected override PlanLayout Layout(StepParameters parameters) => new(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, parameters.South, 10.0, 10.0),
            new[]
            {
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("U1"), "Unit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, parameters.South, 10.0, 8.0)),
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("C1"), "Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, 8.0, 10.0, 10.0)),
            });

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(StepParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
    }
}
```

### Task 2: ADR-015, plans per storey

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: D-095 (a generator may return a different plan per storey; this ADR decides the interface, the aggregators' treatment of storeys that differ, and the terrace designation), the finding of Task 1, ADR-006, ADR-013, ADR-002.
- Produces: ADR-015 (Accepted by the controller under D-098, provisional pending the owner's reading): the options for returning several storeys (every generator returning a list, a storey index parameter, a sibling base class, a new pipeline type) and for carrying them in Grasshopper (a list output wired item-wise, a new Goo), the multiplier question; the decision: `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` with one `PlanLayout` per storey, inputs checked once, each storey built as `PlanGenerator` builds its plan, provenance `Storey` from 0, storey diagnostics prefixed `Storey {k}: `; no aggregator change (a table of the four methods with storeys that differ); a terrace is the part of a ceiling the storey above does not cover, an outdoor roof (D-068); multipliers above 1 allowed (they repeat a storey; the generator's building is the one with every multiplier 1); in Grasshopper a *Plan* list, wired item-wise into an aggregator's *Floors* with *Multipliers* unconnected, each storey previewed at its elevation. Its Decisions line lists D-018 to D-098; the close-out (Task 9) adds D-106, which records it in the decision log.

- [x] **Step 1: Create `docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md` (full file)**

```markdown
# ADR-015: Plans per storey

Status: Accepted (by the controller under D-098; provisional pending the owner's reading)

Date: 2026-10-02

Decisions: D-018, D-068, D-070, D-071, D-072, D-074, D-085, D-095, D-098; applies [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md), [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md), and [ADR-002](ADR-002-geometry.md)

## Context

Through S8.6 a plan generator returns one plan, a single storey; a building with several storeys repeats that plan through the multipliers of the floor aggregators, and the only vertical variation a user can make is to stack plans of different generator runs (different lengths, or plans moved by *Transform Plan*). `SYN-TYP-012` (stepped dwelling bands whose lower roofs become terraces, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)) has a plan that changes from storey to storey: storey `k + 1` is storey `k` stepped back by one terrace depth. D-095 lets a plan generator return one plan per storey and asks for this ADR: the interface, how the floor aggregators treat storeys that differ, and how a terrace is designated (the exposed part of a lower storey's roof is an outdoor roof, the covered part an interzone ceiling, D-068).

What already works, investigated before deciding:

- **The floor aggregators take storeys that differ.** `IFloorAggregator.Aggregate` takes a list of `FloorEntry(Floor, Multiplier)`, bottom to top, and the floors of the entries need not be equal: since S4 a narrower plan may stand on a wider one (a setback) and, since S4.2, a plan may overhang or be moved off the storey below ([ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md)).
- **Terraces are designated on the assembled stack.** `Storeys.Assemble` splits every ceiling by overlap with the zones of the storey above (D-068): the overlap is `Interzone` with the zone above, the rest faces `Outdoors`, which is a roof by `Totals.RoofArea` (a ceiling facing outdoors). A storey whose floor the storey below covers completely is supported, with no `UnsupportedStorey` warning (D-074).
- **Every method keeps that designation.** `Stack` keeps the pieces as they are; `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` keeps ground, roof, and exposed pieces and turns the pieces between storeys adiabatic; `SingleZonePerFloorType` and `SingleZoneBuilding` keep ground, roof, and exposed pieces, keep the pieces between two of their zones `Interzone`, and turn the pieces between two storeys of one zone into internal mass (D-031). Validation compares the ground, roof, and exposed floor area of every method with `Stack` of the same entries.

A characterisation test (`TerraceTests` in `Lod.Core.Tests`) confirms this for three storeys of a band stepping back by 3 m with every aggregator: each lower storey's ceiling has a 30 m² terrace facing outdoors and its covered part between storeys (interzone, adiabatic, or internal mass by method), the roof area equals the ground area (100 m²), no floor is exposed or unsupported, and the building validates; it passes on the code of S8.6 without a change. So `Lod.Core`'s aggregation and validation need no change: what is missing is a generator that returns the storeys, and a way to carry them through Grasshopper.

## Options considered

### How a generator returns several storeys

1. **Every generator returns a list.** `PlanGenerator.Generate` returns one plan per storey for every family, a list of one for the families with one plan. One interface, but every generator, component, and test of the sixteen single-plan families changes for the sake of one family, and a list where one plan is the rule hides that those plans repeat.
2. **A storey index on the family's parameter record.** The family stays a `PlanGenerator`, and the user or component calls it once per storey. No new type, but the storeys of one building are no longer one call: the storey count, the stepping that relates them, and the check that the top storey keeps its dwellings belong to the set, and a component would need a list of storey indices as input.
3. **A sibling base class for generators of several storeys (chosen).** `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` in `Lod.Core.Plans` returns `Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>`, the plans bottom-up, one per storey. The subclass validates its parameters once and lays out every storey (`Layout` returns one `PlanLayout` per storey); the base does for each storey exactly what `PlanGenerator` does for its one plan: preset checks (D-061), surfaces, windows (D-026, ADR-011), and provenance. The two bases share that work in one internal helper, so no rule is duplicated. Each plan is an ordinary `IGeneratedPlan`, so every simplifier, *Transform Plan*, and every aggregator takes it unchanged.
4. **A new pipeline type for a stack of storeys** (`IGeneratedStack`) between generators and aggregators. It would carry the storey structure explicitly, but every simplifier and aggregator would need a second entry point, and it duplicates `FloorEntry`, which already lists storeys bottom-up.

### How Grasshopper carries the storeys

a. **A list output, wired item-wise (chosen).** The generator's *Plan* output is a list, bottom-up. Grasshopper runs a simplifier or *Transform Plan* once per item, so their *Floor* or *Plan* outputs are lists in the same order, and a floor aggregator's *Floors* input, a list, takes them as the storeys bottom-up with *Multipliers* left empty (all 1). No new parameter type, and nothing changes in the simplifier and aggregator components.

b. **A new Goo for a set of storeys.** Explicit, but a new parameter type that every simplifier would have to accept or unpack.

### Multipliers of plans that are storeys

- **Reject a multiplier above 1** for a floor of a multi-storey generator. The aggregators do not know where a floor comes from, so this would need a storey marker on floors, and it would forbid a legitimate building (a band stepping back every second storey).
- **Allow it (chosen).** A multiplier repeats its storey, as for any floor: entries `storey 0 × 2, storey 1 × 1, storey 2 × 2` are a band that steps back above its second and third storeys. The building differs from the generator's (it has more storeys, and its terraces lie higher), so the generator's building is the one with every multiplier 1, which is what the Grasshopper wiring gives by default. Exposure is never multiplied (D-046): with `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` every storey with a terrace is split off as × 1. `TerraceTests` covers this case with every aggregator.

## Decision

### Interface

- `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` (`Lod.Core.Plans`), with the same constructor arguments, `Name`, `Validate`, and `Describe` as `PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>`, and `Layout(TParameters)` returning `IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout>`, one layout per storey, bottom-up, at least one. `Generate(parameters, presets)` checks `FloorHeight`, `OrientationDegrees`, the family's parameters, and every preset slot once, before any storey is laid out, and fails with all of those errors at once; then it builds each storey as `PlanGenerator` builds its plan, and fails if any storey fails.
- Every storey has the same `FloorHeight` and `OrientationDegrees`, the parameters' values. A storey's plan, like every plan, lies at elevation 0; the floor aggregators place storey `k` at the sum of the floor heights below it (D-068).
- **Provenance:** the operation is the generator's `Name`; the parameters are the family's (`Describe`), `FloorHeight`, `OrientationDegrees`, `Presets` as for every plan, non-default limits (ADR-011), and `Storey`, the storey index from 0 at the bottom.
- **Diagnostics:** errors of the parameters and presets are reported once. A diagnostic of one storey (`WindowOmitted`, or an error of its surfaces) keeps its code, severity, and subject, the surface or zone ID within that storey's plan, and its message starts with `Storey {k}: `, so the remarks of several storeys can be told apart.
- `PlanGenerator` is unchanged in behaviour: its plans, diagnostics, and provenance stay identical.

### Floor aggregators

No aggregator changes; each takes the storeys as floor entries with multiplier 1, bottom to top:

| Method | Storeys that differ |
| --- | --- |
| `Stack` | every storey its own, `L{k}/…`; the covered part of a ceiling is `Interzone` with the zone above, its terrace `Outdoors` |
| `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` | every entry has multiplier 1, so every storey is its own group and modelled at its elevation; the result equals `Stack` with every piece between storeys `Adiabatic` (D-075); terraces and roofs kept |
| `SingleZonePerFloorType` | one zone `E{k}` per storey (one floor entry each), one part per storey outline, walls rebuilt per outline; the covered part of a slab lies between `E{k}` and `E{k+1}` (`Interzone` pairs), its terrace `Outdoors`; no internal mass |
| `SingleZoneBuilding` | one zone `BUILDING` with one part per storey outline, walls rebuilt per outline; the covered part of every slab is internal mass, its terrace `Outdoors` |

A terrace is the part of storey `k`'s ceiling that storey `k + 1` does not cover: an outdoor roof piece (D-068). Its walls are the façades of storey `k + 1` above it, outdoor walls with the generator's windows (D-026, ADR-011). Validation is unchanged: `Building.RoofArea` sums every ceiling facing outdoors, terraces included, and is compared with `Stack` of the same entries, as are `Building.GroundArea` and `Building.ExposedFloorArea`. For a band whose every storey stands on the storey below, the roof area (terraces and top roof) equals the ground area, and the exposed floor area is zero.

### Grasshopper

- A generator component of several storeys has the same inputs as the other generator components ([ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md): its count first, the defaults of its `Default` record, the preset inputs in record order, *Preview Location* last) and one output *Plan* with list access, the storeys bottom-up. `TypologyGeneratorComponent` gains the list output for such components; no other component changes.
- **Wiring:** *Plan* (list) → a simplifier (item-wise, a list of floors) → a floor aggregator's *Floors*, with *Multipliers* unconnected (all 1). *Transform Plan* in between moves and rotates every storey alike. Grafting the list, or wiring one storey alone, gives buildings of single storeys, which is the user's choice.
- **Preview:** each storey is previewed at its storey's elevation (the sum of the floor heights below it, in model units) plus the *Preview Location*, so the generator's preview shows the stepped band; the plans' data stay at elevation 0. Simplifier components preview their floors at elevation 0, so the floors of a band overlap there until they are aggregated.

## Consequences

- A family whose plan differs per storey is a `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator`; the sixteen families of one plan and their components are unchanged. `SteppedBandGenerator` (`SYN-TYP-012`, S8.7) is the first such generator.
- A building of storeys that differ is a list of floor entries with multiplier 1; every simplifier and floor aggregator works on it without change, and the terrace designation is that of D-068.
- Multipliers above 1 on such storeys are allowed and repeat a storey; they give another building than the generator's, and the Grasshopper wiring gives multiplier 1 unless the user connects multipliers.
- The narrowest storey of a stepped band is its top storey, so *Perimeter Core*'s precondition (D-087) is decided by the top storey; a band too narrow there fails that simplifier on the top storey only (`PlanTooNarrow`).
- The zone IDs of a storey's plan repeat on every storey (`U1` on each); the aggregators prefix them with `L{k}/` (D-068), so they are unique in the building, as for repeated plans.
- Changing the interface, the provenance key `Storey`, the diagnostic prefix, or the multiplier rule needs a decision-log entry and an update of this ADR.
```

### Task 3: The base of plan generators with one plan per storey

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Core/Plans/MultiStoreyPlanGenerator.cs`, `src/Lod.Core/Plans/PlanBuilder.cs` (internal)
- Modify: `src/Lod.Core/Plans/PlanGenerator.cs` (S2 file, last changed in S8.1)
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Core.Tests/Plans/MultiStoreyPlanGeneratorTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` (S2–S8.1: `Generate`, `Validate`, `Layout`, `Describe`, `IsPositive`, `Tolerances`, `Limits`, `GeometryLimits`), `PlanParameters`, `PlanPresets`, `PlanLayout`, `PlannedZone`, `Result`, `Diagnostic` (a record, so `with { Message = … }`), `DiagnosticCodes`, `Provenance`, `ExampleResidentialPresets`.
- Produces: `public abstract class MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets> where TParameters : PlanParameters where TPresets : PlanPresets` with `public const string StoreyKey = "Storey"`, the constructors `(ToleranceSettings)` and `(ToleranceSettings, GeometryLimits)`, `public abstract string Name`, `protected ToleranceSettings Tolerances`, `protected GeometryLimits Limits`, `public Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> Generate(TParameters, TPresets)`, `protected abstract IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TParameters)`, `protected abstract IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(TParameters)` (bottom-up, at least one), `protected abstract IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TParameters)`, and `protected static bool IsPositive(double)`. `Generate` checks `FloorHeight`, `OrientationDegrees`, `Validate`, and every preset slot once and returns all errors before `Layout` is called; then builds every storey with `PlanBuilder.Build`, appending `Storey=k` to the described parameters, prefixes every storey diagnostic with `Storey {k}: ` (code, severity, and subject unchanged), and fails if any storey has an error. `internal static class PlanBuilder` with `CheckInputs(PlanParameters, IEnumerable<Diagnostic>, PlanPresets)`, `Build(string name, PlanLayout, PlanParameters, PlanPresets, IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>>, GeometryLimits, ToleranceSettings)`, and `IsPositive(double)`: the code of S8.1's `PlanGenerator.Generate` (common checks, preset slots, zones, surfaces, windows, provenance with the non-default limits), moved unchanged. `PlanGenerator.Generate` calls `PlanBuilder.CheckInputs` and `PlanBuilder.Build`; its public and protected members and its outputs are unchanged.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Core.Tests/Plans/MultiStoreyPlanGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

6 tests on a private band generator of a 4 m unit `U1` and a hall `C1`, 1 m shallower on every storey above the first: the storeys bottom-up as ordinary plans at elevation 0 with the parameters' floor height and orientation; provenance (the generator's name, the family's parameters, `FloorHeight`, `OrientationDegrees`, `Presets`, `Storey`); storey 0 equal to the plan of a `PlanGenerator` of the same layout, provenance aside from `Storey=0`; parameter and preset errors reported once and before any layout (`Layouts` stays 0); with a 0.5 m hall, the four `WindowOmitted` remarks keep their code, severity, and subject (a wall of that storey's plan) and start with `Storey 0: ` or `Storey 1: `; a storey whose zones overlap fails the generation, every error starting with `Storey 1: `.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Core.Tests.Plans;

/// <summary>The base of generators that return one plan per storey (D-095, ADR-015).</summary>
public sealed class MultiStoreyPlanGeneratorTests
{
    private readonly BandGenerator _generator = new();

    [Fact]
    public void EveryStoreyIsOnePlanBottomUp()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(3), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(3, plans.Count);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 32.0, 24.0, 16.0 }, plans.Select(p => p.Footprint.Area));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(new[] { "U1", "C1" }, p.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 16.0, 12.0, 8.0 }, plans.Select(p => p.Zones[0].FloorArea));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(3.0, p.FloorHeight));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(12.5, p.OrientationDegrees));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.All(p.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(0.0, z.Parts[0].Elevation)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsTheGeneratorAndTheStoreyIndex()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(nameof(BandGenerator), p.Provenance.Operation));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { "FloorHeight=3", "HallWidth=4", "OrientationDegrees=12.5", "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Corridor:Example Corridor", "Storey=0", "Storeys=2" },
            plans[0].Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
        Assert.Equal("1", plans[1].Provenance.Parameters.Single(p => p.Key == "Storey").Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AStoreyIsBuiltExactlyAsAPlanGeneratorBuildsItsPlan()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan storey = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value[0];
        IGeneratedPlan plan = new GroundStoreyGenerator().Generate(new BandParameters(2), Presets()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(plan), TextReport.Describe(storey));
        Assert.Equal(
            plan.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}").Append("Storey=0").OrderBy(p => p, System.StringComparer.Ordinal),
            storey.Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ParameterAndPresetErrorsAreReportedOnceBeforeAnyStoreyIsLaidOut()
    {
        var generator = new BandGenerator();

        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = generator.Generate(new BandParameters(0, FloorHeight: -1.0), Presets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair));

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch },
            result.Diagnostics.Select(d => d.Code));
        Assert.StartsWith("FloorHeight must be positive", result.Diagnostics[0].Message);
        Assert.Equal("Storeys must be at least 1, got 0.", result.Diagnostics[1].Message);
        Assert.Equal(0, generator.Layouts);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ADiagnosticOfOneStoreyNamesItsStorey()
    {
        // A 0.5 m hall: the hall's south and north walls of every storey are below ADR-011's 1 m minimum and get no window.
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(2, HallWidth: 0.5), Presets());

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic[] omitted = result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(4, omitted.Length);
        Assert.All(omitted, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticSeverity.Info, d.Severity));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Storey 0: ", "Storey 0: ", "Storey 1: ", "Storey 1: " }, omitted.Select(d => d.Message.Substring(0, 10)));
        IGeneratedPlan[] plans = result.Value.ToArray();
        Assert.All(omitted.Take(2), d => Assert.Contains(plans[0].Surfaces, s => s.Id.Value == d.Subject));
        Assert.Equal(
            "Storey 1: Outdoor wall C1/W1 is 0.5 m long, below the 1 m minimum for a window (ADR-011); it has no window (0.3 m² of glazing not placed).",
            omitted[2].Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AStoreyWhoseZonesDoNotTileFailsTheGenerationAndIsNamed()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(new BandParameters(3, OverlapStorey: 1), Presets());

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Assert.NotEmpty(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error), d => Assert.StartsWith("Storey 1: ", d.Message));
    }

    private static BandPresets Presets(ProgramPreset? unit = null) =>
        new(unit ?? ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);

    /// <param name="Storeys">Number of storeys, at least 1.</param>
    /// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
    /// <param name="HallWidth">Width of the hall along x, m.</param>
    /// <param name="OverlapStorey">A storey whose hall overlaps its unit by 0.5 m, or −1 for none.</param>
    private sealed record BandParameters(int Storeys, double FloorHeight = 3.0, double HallWidth = 4.0, int OverlapStorey = -1)
        : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, 12.5);

    private sealed record BandPresets(ProgramPreset Unit, ProgramPreset Hall) : PlanPresets
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
        {
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Unit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Unit),
            new PresetSlot(nameof(Hall), SpaceType.Corridor, Hall),
        };
    }

    private static PlanLayout Storey(BandParameters parameters, int k)
    {
        double south = k;
        double hallWest = k == parameters.OverlapStorey ? 3.5 : 4.0;
        return new PlanLayout(
            Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, south, 4.0 + parameters.HallWidth, 4.0),
            new[]
            {
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("U1"), "Unit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, south, 4.0, 4.0)),
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("C1"), "Hall", SpaceType.Corridor, Polygon2.Rectangle(hallWest, south, 4.0 + parameters.HallWidth, 4.0)),
            });
    }

    private static IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Described(BandParameters parameters) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(BandParameters.Storeys), parameters.Storeys.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(BandParameters.HallWidth), Provenance.Format(parameters.HallWidth)),
    };

    /// <summary>A band of a 4 m unit <c>U1</c> and a hall <c>C1</c> east of it, 4 m deep on storey 0 and 1 m shallower on every storey above.</summary>
    private sealed class BandGenerator : MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<BandParameters, BandPresets>
    {
        public BandGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public int Layouts { get; private set; }

        public override string Name => nameof(BandGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(BandParameters parameters)
        {
            if (parameters.Storeys < 1)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Storeys must be at least 1, got {parameters.Storeys}.");
            }
        }

        protected override IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(BandParameters parameters)
        {
            Layouts++;
            return Enumerable.Range(0, parameters.Storeys).Select(k => Storey(parameters, k)).ToArray();
        }

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(BandParameters parameters) => Described(parameters);
    }

    /// <summary>Storey 0 of <see cref="BandGenerator"/> as a plan generator of one plan.</summary>
    private sealed class GroundStoreyGenerator : PlanGenerator<BandParameters, BandPresets>
    {
        public GroundStoreyGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public override string Name => nameof(BandGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(BandParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<Diagnostic>();

        protected override PlanLayout Layout(BandParameters parameters) => Storey(parameters, 0);

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(BandParameters parameters) => Described(parameters);
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — create `src/Lod.Core/Plans/PlanBuilder.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Layout;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>
/// The work shared by <see cref="PlanGenerator{TParameters, TPresets}"/> and <see cref="MultiStoreyPlanGenerator{TParameters, TPresets}"/>: the
/// checks of the common parameters and of every preset slot (D-061), and building one plan from a layout: zones with their presets, surfaces,
/// centred windows within the minimum wall and window geometry (D-026, ADR-011), and provenance.
/// </summary>
internal static class PlanBuilder
{
    /// <summary>Checks <c>FloorHeight</c> and <c>OrientationDegrees</c>, then adds the family's errors and the preset slot errors, in that order.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="familyErrors">Errors of the family's own parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Presets.</param>
    /// <returns>Every error, empty when the inputs are valid.</returns>
    public static List<Diagnostic> CheckInputs(PlanParameters parameters, IEnumerable<Diagnostic> familyErrors, PlanPresets presets)
    {
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        if (!IsPositive(parameters.FloorHeight))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"FloorHeight must be positive, got {parameters.FloorHeight}."));
        }

        if (double.IsNaN(parameters.OrientationDegrees) || double.IsInfinity(parameters.OrientationDegrees))
        {
            diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"OrientationDegrees must be finite, got {parameters.OrientationDegrees}."));
        }

        diagnostics.AddRange(familyErrors);
        diagnostics.AddRange(CheckSlots(presets.Slots));
        return diagnostics;
    }

    /// <summary>Builds one plan from a layout of valid parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="name">Operation name recorded in provenance.</param>
    /// <param name="layout">Footprint and zones.</param>
    /// <param name="parameters">Validated parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Checked presets.</param>
    /// <param name="described">The family's parameters for provenance, and any further keys of the caller.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011); recorded in provenance when not the default.</param>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <returns>The plan, or errors.</returns>
    public static Result<IGeneratedPlan> Build(
        string name,
        PlanLayout layout,
        PlanParameters parameters,
        PlanPresets presets,
        IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> described,
        GeometryLimits limits,
        ToleranceSettings tolerances)
    {
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        var zones = new List<Zone>();
        foreach (PlannedZone planned in layout.Zones)
        {
            ProgramPreset? preset = Find(presets, planned.SpaceType);
            if (preset is null)
            {
                diagnostics.Add(Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset, $"No program preset for space type {planned.SpaceType}.", planned.Id.Value));
                continue;
            }

            zones.Add(new Zone(planned.Id, planned.Name, planned.SpaceType, new[] { new ZonePart(planned.Footprint, 0.0, parameters.FloorHeight) }, preset.Program, Enumerable.Empty<ZoneId>()));
        }

        if (diagnostics.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(diagnostics);
        }

        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> surfaces = new LayoutSurfaceBuilder(tolerances)
            .Build(layout.Footprint, layout.Zones.Select(z => new LayoutZone(z.Id, z.Footprint)).ToArray(), parameters.FloorHeight);
        if (!surfaces.IsSuccess)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(surfaces.Diagnostics);
        }

        Dictionary<ZoneId, double> ratios = layout.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id, z => Find(presets, z.SpaceType)!.WindowToWallRatio);
        Result<IReadOnlyList<Surface>> withWindows = LayoutMeasures.PlaceWindowsByRatio(surfaces.Value, id => ratios[id], limits, tolerances);
        IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> recorded = described
            .Concat(new[]
            {
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(PlanParameters.FloorHeight), Provenance.Format(parameters.FloorHeight)),
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(PlanParameters.OrientationDegrees), Provenance.Format(parameters.OrientationDegrees)),
                new KeyValuePair<string, string>("Presets", string.Join(";", presets.Slots.Select(s => $"{s.Preset.SpaceType}:{s.Preset.Name}"))),
            })
            .Concat(DescribeLimits(limits));
        var plan = new GeneratedPlan(layout.Footprint, parameters.FloorHeight, parameters.OrientationDegrees, zones, withWindows.Value, Provenance.Of(name, recorded));
        return Result.Success<IGeneratedPlan>(plan, withWindows.Diagnostics);
    }

    /// <summary>Whether a value is finite and positive.</summary>
    /// <param name="value">The value.</param>
    /// <returns><c>true</c> when positive and finite.</returns>
    public static bool IsPositive(double value) => value > 0 && !double.IsInfinity(value);

    private static IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> DescribeLimits(GeometryLimits limits) => limits == GeometryLimits.Default
        ? Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>()
        : new[]
        {
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowWallLength), Provenance.Format(limits.MinimumWindowWallLength)),
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowWidth), Provenance.Format(limits.MinimumWindowWidth)),
            new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(GeometryLimits.MinimumWindowHeight), Provenance.Format(limits.MinimumWindowHeight)),
        };

    private static IEnumerable<Diagnostic> CheckSlots(IEnumerable<PresetSlot> slots)
    {
        foreach (PresetSlot slot in slots)
        {
            if (slot.Preset is null)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.MissingPreset, $"Input {slot.Input} needs a {slot.SpaceType} preset, got none.", slot.Input);
            }
            else if (slot.Preset.SpaceType != slot.SpaceType)
            {
                yield return Diagnostic.Error(
                    DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch,
                    $"Input {slot.Input} needs a {slot.SpaceType} preset, got '{slot.Preset.Name}' ({slot.Preset.SpaceType}).",
                    slot.Input);
            }
        }
    }

    private static ProgramPreset? Find(PlanPresets presets, SpaceType spaceType) =>
        presets.Slots.FirstOrDefault(s => s.SpaceType == spaceType)?.Preset;
}
```

`Generate` now calls `PlanBuilder`; the private helpers that moved there are removed, and the class's members, documentation, and behaviour are otherwise unchanged.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>Parameters every plan generator needs. Typology-specific parameters extend this record (spec §6).</summary>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public abstract record PlanParameters(double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees);

/// <summary>A zone proposed by a generator's layout step.</summary>
/// <param name="Id">Zone ID.</param>
/// <param name="Name">Display name.</param>
/// <param name="SpaceType">Space type; selects the program preset.</param>
/// <param name="Footprint">Zone polygon.</param>
public sealed record PlannedZone(ZoneId Id, string Name, SpaceType SpaceType, Polygon2 Footprint);

/// <summary>The geometric output of a generator's layout step.</summary>
/// <param name="Footprint">Plan boundary.</param>
/// <param name="Zones">Zones tiling the footprint.</param>
public sealed record PlanLayout(Polygon2 Footprint, IReadOnlyList<PlannedZone> Zones);

/// <summary>
/// Base class of plan generators (spec §5). Subclasses only validate their parameters and lay out zones; this class assigns program presets,
/// builds surfaces, places centered windows from each preset's WWR within the minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011), and records provenance.
/// Generation is deterministic.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="TParameters">The generator's parameter record.</typeparam>
/// <typeparam name="TPresets">The generator's preset record, one preset per space type it places (D-061).</typeparam>
public abstract class PlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>
    where TParameters : PlanParameters
    where TPresets : PlanPresets
{
    /// <summary>Initialises the generator with the default minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    protected PlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : this(tolerances, GeometryLimits.Default)
    {
    }

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011); recorded in provenance when not the default.</param>
    protected PlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances, GeometryLimits limits)
    {
        Tolerances = tolerances;
        Limits = limits;
    }

    /// <summary>Operation name recorded in provenance.</summary>
    public abstract string Name { get; }

    /// <summary>Tolerances.</summary>
    protected ToleranceSettings Tolerances { get; }

    /// <summary>Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    protected GeometryLimits Limits { get; }

    /// <summary>Generates a detailed plan.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Generator parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Program presets, one per slot; each must have its slot's space type.</param>
    /// <returns>The plan, or errors.</returns>
    public Result<IGeneratedPlan> Generate(TParameters parameters, TPresets presets)
    {
        List<Diagnostic> errors = PlanBuilder.CheckInputs(parameters, Validate(parameters), presets);
        if (errors.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IGeneratedPlan>(errors);
        }

        return PlanBuilder.Build(Name, Layout(parameters), parameters, presets, Describe(parameters), Limits, Tolerances);
    }

    /// <summary>Checks typology-specific parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Errors, if any.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Lays out zones for valid parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Validated parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Footprint and zones.</returns>
    protected abstract PlanLayout Layout(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Typology-specific parameters for provenance, as invariant-culture text.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Key-value pairs.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Whether a value is finite and positive.</summary>
    /// <param name="value">The value.</param>
    /// <returns><c>true</c> when positive and finite.</returns>
    protected static bool IsPositive(double value) => PlanBuilder.IsPositive(value);
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement — create `src/Lod.Core/Plans/MultiStoreyPlanGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;

namespace Lod.Core.Plans;

/// <summary>
/// Base class of plan generators whose plan differs per storey (D-095, ADR-015). The subclass validates its parameters once and lays out every
/// storey; this class checks the common parameters and the presets once, then builds every storey exactly as
/// <see cref="PlanGenerator{TParameters, TPresets}"/> builds its plan (presets, surfaces, centred windows within the minimum wall and window geometry
/// of ADR-011, provenance) and returns the plans bottom-up. Each plan records its storey index as the provenance parameter <c>Storey</c>, from 0 at
/// the bottom. The floor aggregators stack the plans as floor entries with multiplier 1 (ADR-015). Generation is deterministic.
/// </summary>
/// <typeparam name="TParameters">The generator's parameter record.</typeparam>
/// <typeparam name="TPresets">The generator's preset record, one preset per space type it places (D-061).</typeparam>
public abstract class MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>
    where TParameters : PlanParameters
    where TPresets : PlanPresets
{
    /// <summary>Provenance key of the storey index.</summary>
    public const string StoreyKey = "Storey";

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator with the default minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    protected MultiStoreyPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : this(tolerances, GeometryLimits.Default)
    {
    }

    /// <summary>Initialises the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="limits">Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011); recorded in provenance when not the default.</param>
    protected MultiStoreyPlanGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances, GeometryLimits limits)
    {
        Tolerances = tolerances;
        Limits = limits;
    }

    /// <summary>Operation name recorded in provenance.</summary>
    public abstract string Name { get; }

    /// <summary>Tolerances.</summary>
    protected ToleranceSettings Tolerances { get; }

    /// <summary>Minimum wall and window geometry (ADR-011).</summary>
    protected GeometryLimits Limits { get; }

    /// <summary>Generates the detailed plan of every storey.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Generator parameters.</param>
    /// <param name="presets">Program presets, one per slot; each must have its slot's space type.</param>
    /// <returns>One plan per storey, bottom-up, or errors. A diagnostic of one storey has a message starting with <c>Storey {k}: </c>.</returns>
    public Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> Generate(TParameters parameters, TPresets presets)
    {
        List<Diagnostic> errors = PlanBuilder.CheckInputs(parameters, Validate(parameters), presets);
        if (errors.Count > 0)
        {
            return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(errors);
        }

        var plans = new List<IGeneratedPlan>();
        var diagnostics = new List<Diagnostic>();
        IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> layouts = Layout(parameters);
        for (int k = 0; k < layouts.Count; k++)
        {
            IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> described = Describe(parameters)
                .Append(new KeyValuePair<string, string>(StoreyKey, k.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)));
            Result<IGeneratedPlan> storey = PlanBuilder.Build(Name, layouts[k], parameters, presets, described, Limits, Tolerances);
            string prefix = $"Storey {k}: ";
            diagnostics.AddRange(storey.Diagnostics.Select(d => d with { Message = prefix + d.Message }));
            if (storey.IsSuccess)
            {
                plans.Add(storey.Value);
            }
        }

        return diagnostics.Any(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error)
            ? Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(diagnostics)
            : Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>(plans.AsReadOnly(), diagnostics);
    }

    /// <summary>Checks typology-specific parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Errors, if any.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Lays out every storey for valid parameters.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Validated parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Footprint and zones of every storey, bottom-up; at least one.</returns>
    protected abstract IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Typology-specific parameters for provenance, as invariant-culture text; the same for every storey.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Key-value pairs.</returns>
    protected abstract IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(TParameters parameters);

    /// <summary>Whether a value is finite and positive.</summary>
    /// <param name="value">The value.</param>
    /// <returns><c>true</c> when positive and finite.</returns>
    protected static bool IsPositive(double value) => PlanBuilder.IsPositive(value);
}
```

## Slice B — `feature/stepped-band`

The family, test-first: the generator tests first (they fail to build), the generator, the snapshot of the three canonical storeys reviewed and accepted; then the integration matrix, which passes at once; if a case of the matrix fails, stop and report: a pipeline change needs its own test-first task.

### Task 4: Stepped dwelling bands with exposed terraces (`SYN-TYP-012`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/SteppedBand/SteppedBandGenerator.cs`
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/SteppedBand/SteppedBandGeneratorTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/SteppedBand/Snapshots/stepped-band-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` (Task 3: `Generate`, `Validate`, `Layout`, `Describe`, `IsPositive`, `StoreyKey`), `PlanParameters`, `PlanPresets.Slots`, `PresetSlot`, `PlanLayout`, `PlannedZone(ZoneId, string, SpaceType, Polygon2)`, `Polygon2.Rectangle`, `Provenance.Format`, `Diagnostic.Error`, `DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter`, `PresetSpaceTypeMismatch`, `WindowOmitted`; `SpaceType.DwellingUnit`, `Corridor`, and `Stair`, `ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit`, `Corridor`, and `Stair`; test support `PlanAssertions` (`TilesTheFootprint`, `HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall`, `Neighbours`), `Snapshot.Match`, `TextReport.Describe`, `PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default).IntersectionArea`, `Orientation.Bin`.
- Produces: `public sealed record SteppedBandParameters(int StoreyCount, double Length, double BaseDepth, double StepDepth, double CorridorWidth, double UnitWidth, double StairWidth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (3, 36, 14, 3, 2, 6, 4, 3, 0); `public sealed record SteppedBandPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Corridor, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets` (slots in that order); `public sealed class SteppedBandGenerator(ToleranceSettings) : MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<SteppedBandParameters, SteppedBandPresets>`, `Name` = `SteppedBandGenerator`, `public const int MinimumStoreys = 2`, `public static int DwellingsPerStorey(SteppedBandParameters)` = max(1, round half away from zero of (`Length` − `StairWidth`) / `UnitWidth`). Storey `k` (from 0, bottom-up): footprint `[0, Length] × [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth + CorridorWidth]`; `ST` (*Stair*, `Stair`) `[0, StairWidth] × [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth]`; `CO` (*Corridor*, `Corridor`) `[0, Length] × [BaseDepth, BaseDepth + CorridorWidth]`; `U1…Un` (*Unit i*, `DwellingUnit`) dividing `[StairWidth, Length] × [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth]` evenly, west to east, the last ending exactly at `Length`. Errors `InvalidParameter`, all at once: fewer than 2 storeys (`StoreyCount must be at least 2 (the band steps back from storey to storey, R001), got 1.`), each length that is not positive and finite (`{name} must be positive, got {value}.`); and, only when every length is positive, `Length` not above `StairWidth` (`Length 4 m must exceed StairWidth 4 m, so that the band has dwellings beside the stair (R002).`, a check ADR-006 does not list, recorded in Task 7 and D-106) and, when the count is valid, `BaseDepth − (StoreyCount − 1)·StepDepth ≤ 0` (`BaseDepth 12 m leaves no dwelling depth on the top storey: its 4 steps of StepDepth 3 m take 12 m; SYN-TYP-012 needs dwellings on every storey, so reduce StoreyCount or StepDepth, or increase BaseDepth.`); no upper bound; provenance keys `StoreyCount`, `Length`, `BaseDepth`, `StepDepth`, `CorridorWidth`, `UnitWidth`, `StairWidth`, and `Storey` (Task 3). By default three storeys of 576, 468, and 360 m² (1404 m²), 7 zones each: `ST` 56, 44, and 32 m², `CO` 72 m², five dwellings of 6.4 m × 14, 11, and 8 m (89.6, 70.4, and 51.2 m²); every storey stands on the one below and leaves it a 36 m × 3 m terrace (108 m²); 11 windows per storey (33 in all) with 70.8, 67.2, and 63.6 m² of glazing (201.6 m²), including the south walls of `ST` and `U1` to `U5` that face the terraces; no diagnostic.
