- [x] **Step 2: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/SteppedBand/SteppedBandGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

36 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical band (three storeys, zone IDs, names, space types, rectangles, areas); storey `k` stepped back `k` times (storeys 0 to 2); tiling of every storey for 2, 3, and 5 storeys; the dwellings dividing the band evenly for four lengths; every dwelling adjoining the corridor along the rear over 6.4 m, on every storey; every storey covered by the storey below, which keeps a 108 m² terrace (2 to 4 storeys); the windows (11 per storey, the walls facing the terraces included) and no diagnostic; a 0.8 m corridor giving six `WindowOmitted` remarks, two per storey, named by their storey; eleven invalid parameter sets, each a single error; a storey count of 1; the top storey without dwelling depth; the stair without room for dwellings; preset slots and swapped presets reported once; determinism; provenance with the storey; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.SteppedBand;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.SteppedBand;

public sealed class SteppedBandGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly SteppedBandPresets Examples = new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    private readonly SteppedBandGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new SteppedBandParameters(
                StoreyCount: 3,
                Length: 36.0,
                BaseDepth: 14.0,
                StepDepth: 3.0,
                CorridorWidth: 2.0,
                UnitWidth: 6.0,
                StairWidth: 4.0,
                FloorHeight: 3.0,
                OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            SteppedBandParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalBandHasThreeStoreysOfAStairACorridorAndFiveDwellings()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(3, plans.Count);
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST", "CO", "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4", "U5" }, p.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(new[] { "Stair", "Corridor", "Unit 1", "Unit 2", "Unit 3", "Unit 4", "Unit 5" }, p.Zones.Select(z => z.Name)));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(
            new[] { SpaceType.Stair, SpaceType.Corridor, SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.DwellingUnit },
            p.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 576.0, 468.0, 360.0 }, plans.Select(p => System.Math.Round(p.Footprint.Area, 9)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 89.6, 70.4, 51.2 }, plans.Select(p => System.Math.Round(Zone(p, "U3").FloorArea, 9)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 56.0, 44.0, 32.0 }, plans.Select(p => System.Math.Round(Zone(p, "ST").FloorArea, 9)));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(72.0, Zone(p, "CO").FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.All(p.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(3.0, z.Parts[0].Height)));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.All(p.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(0.0, z.Parts[0].Elevation)));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Empty(p.Footprint.Holes));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0)]
    [InlineData(1)]
    [InlineData(2)]
    public void StoreyKIsTheBandSteppedBackKTimes(int k)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default)[k];
        double south = 3.0 * k;

        AssertRectangle(0.0, south, 36.0, 16.0, plan.Footprint.Outer.ToArray());
        AssertRectangle(0.0, south, 4.0, 14.0, Outer(plan, "ST"));
        AssertRectangle(0.0, 14.0, 36.0, 16.0, Outer(plan, "CO"));
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
        {
            AssertRectangle(4.0 + (6.4 * i), south, i == 4 ? 36.0 : 4.0 + (6.4 * (i + 1)), 14.0, Outer(plan, $"U{i + 1}"));
        }
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(5)]
    public void EveryStoreyIsTiledByItsZones(int storeys)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default with { StoreyCount = storeys, BaseDepth = 16.0 });

        Assert.Equal(storeys, plans.Count);
        Assert.All(plans, PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(10.0, 1)]
    [InlineData(36.0, 5)]
    [InlineData(37.0, 6)]
    [InlineData(64.0, 10)]
    public void TheDwellingsDivideTheBandEvenly(double length, int dwellings)
    {
        // (Length − StairWidth) / UnitWidth rounded half away from zero, at least 1: 6/6 → 1, 32/6 → 5, 33/6 = 5.5 → 6, 60/6 → 10.
        SteppedBandParameters parameters = SteppedBandParameters.Default with { Length = length };

        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Generate(parameters);

        Assert.Equal(dwellings, SteppedBandGenerator.DwellingsPerStorey(parameters));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(dwellings, p.Zones.Count(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit)));
        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.All(p.Zones.Where(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit), z => Assert.Equal((length - 4.0) / dwellings, z.Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.Max(v => v.X) - z.Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.Min(v => v.X), 9)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryDwellingAdjoinsTheCorridorAlongTheRearOnEveryStorey()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default);

        foreach (IGeneratedPlan plan in plans)
        {
            Assert.Equal(new[] { "CO", "U1" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "ST"));
            Assert.Equal(new[] { "CO", "ST", "U2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U1"));
            Assert.Equal(new[] { "CO", "U2", "U4" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U3"));
            Assert.Equal(new[] { "CO", "U4" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "U5"));
            Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST", "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4", "U5" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "CO"));
            foreach (string unit in new[] { "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4", "U5" })
            {
                WallSurface rear = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Single(w => w.Zone.Value == unit && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == "CO");
                Assert.Equal(6.4, rear.Length, 9);
            }
        }
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(4)]
    public void EveryStoreyIsCoveredByTheStoreyBelowWhichKeepsATerrace(int storeys)
    {
        // Length × StepDepth of every lower storey is not covered by the storey above: the terrace of 36 m × 3 m.
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default with { StoreyCount = storeys, BaseDepth = 14.0 });
        var ops = new PolygonOps(ToleranceSettings.Default);

        for (int k = 1; k < plans.Count; k++)
        {
            Polygon2 upper = plans[k].Footprint;
            Polygon2 lower = plans[k - 1].Footprint;
            Assert.Equal(upper.Area, ops.IntersectionArea(upper, lower), 9);
            Assert.Equal(36.0 * 3.0, lower.Area - upper.Area, 9);
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPresetIncludingTheWallsFacingTheTerraces()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.All(result.Value, p => PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(p, Examples));
        Assert.All(result.Value, p => Assert.Equal(11, p.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.Windows.Count)));
        for (int k = 1; k < 3; k++)
        {
            // The south façade of storey k stands on the terrace of storey k − 1 at y = 3k: the stair's and every dwelling's south wall.
            WallSurface[] terraceFacing = result.Value[k].Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
                .Where(w => w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && Orientation.Bin(w.PlanAzimuth) == OrientationBin.South)
                .ToArray();
            Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST", "U1", "U2", "U3", "U4", "U5" }, terraceFacing.Select(w => w.Zone.Value).OrderBy(z => z, System.StringComparer.Ordinal));
            Assert.All(terraceFacing, w => Assert.True(w.Start.Y == 3.0 * k && w.End.Y == 3.0 * k));
            Assert.All(terraceFacing, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FacadesBelowTheMinimumWallLengthGetNoWindowAndNameTheirStorey()
    {
        // 0.8 m corridor: its 0.8 m west and east ends are below ADR-011's 1 m minimum on every storey.
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default with { CorridorWidth = 0.8 }, Examples);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic[] omitted = result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(6, omitted.Length);
        Assert.All(omitted, d => Assert.StartsWith("CO/", d.Subject));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 2 }, omitted.Select(d => int.Parse(d.Message.Substring(7, 1), System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(1, 36.0, 14.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, "StoreyCount")]
    [InlineData(0, 36.0, 14.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, "StoreyCount")]
    [InlineData(3, 0.0, 14.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, "Length")]
    [InlineData(3, 36.0, -1.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, "BaseDepth")]
    [InlineData(3, 36.0, 14.0, 0.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, "StepDepth")]
    [InlineData(3, 36.0, 14.0, 3.0, double.NaN, 6.0, 4.0, "CorridorWidth")]
    [InlineData(3, 36.0, 14.0, 3.0, 2.0, 0.0, 4.0, "UnitWidth")]
    [InlineData(3, 36.0, 14.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, double.PositiveInfinity, "StairWidth")]
    [InlineData(3, 4.0, 14.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, "Length")]
    [InlineData(5, 36.0, 12.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, "BaseDepth")]
    [InlineData(3, 36.0, 5.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, "BaseDepth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(int storeys, double length, double baseDepth, double stepDepth, double corridorWidth, double unitWidth, double stairWidth, string name)
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(
            new SteppedBandParameters(storeys, length, baseDepth, stepDepth, corridorWidth, unitWidth, stairWidth, 3.0, 0.0),
            Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OneStoreyIsNotAStep()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default with { StoreyCount = 1 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal("StoreyCount must be at least 2 (the band steps back from storey to storey, R001), got 1.", Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics).Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheTopStoreyMustKeepItsDwellings()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default with { StoreyCount = 5, BaseDepth = 12.0 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal(
            "BaseDepth 12 m leaves no dwelling depth on the top storey: its 4 steps of StepDepth 3 m take 12 m; SYN-TYP-012 needs dwellings on every storey, so reduce StoreyCount or StepDepth, or increase BaseDepth.",
            Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics).Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheStairMustLeaveRoomForDwellings()
    {
        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default with { Length = 4.0 }, Examples);

        Assert.Equal("Length 4 m must exceed StairWidth 4 m, so that the band has dwellings beside the stair (R002).", Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics).Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeAndAreCheckedOnce()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("DwellingUnit", SpaceType.DwellingUnit), ("Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor), ("Stair", SpaceType.Stair) },
            Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.DwellingUnit, Examples.Corridor, Examples.Stair }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
        var swapped = new SteppedBandPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor, ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit);

        Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result = _generator.Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default, swapped);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "DwellingUnit", "Stair" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(Describe(Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default)), Describe(Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersPresetsAndTheStorey()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default);

        Assert.All(plans, p => Assert.Equal(nameof(SteppedBandGenerator), p.Provenance.Operation));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "BaseDepth=14", "CorridorWidth=2", "FloorHeight=3", "Length=36", "OrientationDegrees=0",
                "Presets=DwellingUnit:Example Dwelling Unit;Corridor:Example Corridor;Stair:Example Stair", "StairWidth=4", "StepDepth=3", "Storey=0",
                "StoreyCount=3", "UnitWidth=6",
            },
            plans[0].Provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "0", "1", "2" }, plans.Select(p => p.Provenance.Parameters.Single(q => q.Key == "Storey").Value));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalStoreysSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(Describe(Generate(SteppedBandParameters.Default)), "stepped-band-canonical");
    }

    /// <summary>The text report of every storey, bottom-up, each after a line <c>storey k</c>.</summary>
    private static string Describe(IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans)
    {
        var builder = new StringBuilder();
        for (int k = 0; k < plans.Count; k++)
        {
            builder.Append("storey ").Append(k).Append('\n').Append(TextReport.Describe(plans[k]));
        }

        return builder.ToString();
    }

    private static void AssertRectangle(double minX, double minY, double maxX, double maxY, Point2[] actual)
    {
        Point2[] expected = Polygon2.Rectangle(minX, minY, maxX, maxY).Outer.ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(expected.Length, actual.Length);
        for (int i = 0; i < expected.Length; i++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(expected[i].X, actual[i].X, 9);
            Assert.Equal(expected[i].Y, actual[i].Y, 9);
        }
    }

    private static Zone Zone(IGeneratedPlan plan, string id) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == id);

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => Zone(plan, zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> Generate(SteppedBandParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/SteppedBand/SteppedBandGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Generators.SteppedBand;

/// <summary>Parameters of the stepped dwelling band with exposed terraces (SYN-TYP-012, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="StoreyCount">Number of storeys; at least 2.</param>
/// <param name="Length">Band length along plan x, m.</param>
/// <param name="BaseDepth">Dwelling depth on the ground storey, from the south façade to the corridor, m.</param>
/// <param name="StepDepth">Depth by which every storey steps back from the one below, the terrace depth, m.</param>
/// <param name="CorridorWidth">Width of the corridor along the band's rear (plan north), m.</param>
/// <param name="UnitWidth">Target dwelling width along the band, m; adjusted so the dwellings fill the band.</param>
/// <param name="StairWidth">Width of the stair at the west end, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record SteppedBandParameters(
    int StoreyCount,
    double Length,
    double BaseDepth,
    double StepDepth,
    double CorridorWidth,
    double UnitWidth,
    double StairWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The ADR-006 defaults: 3 storeys of a 36 m band, 14 m of dwelling depth on the ground storey stepping back 3 m per storey, a 2 m rear
    /// corridor, 6 m target dwellings, a 4 m stair, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.
    /// </summary>
    public static SteppedBandParameters Default { get; } = new(3, 36.0, 14.0, 3.0, 2.0, 6.0, 4.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the stepped band, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="DwellingUnit">Preset of the dwellings; space type <see cref="SpaceType.DwellingUnit"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Corridor">Preset of the rear corridor; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Corridor"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Stair">Preset of the stair; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Stair"/>.</param>
public sealed record SteppedBandPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit, ProgramPreset Corridor, ProgramPreset Stair) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Corridor), SpaceType.Corridor, Corridor),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Stair), SpaceType.Stair, Stair),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Stepped dwelling bands with exposed terraces (SYN-TYP-012, ADR-006): the band steps back storey by storey so that the part of each lower roof
/// that the storey above leaves free is a terrace, an outdoor roof (R001, D-068), and every dwelling adjoins the shared corridor along the band's
/// rear on every storey (R002). One plan per storey (D-095, ADR-015), bottom-up; one zone per dwelling and storey (D-085).
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// On storey <c>k</c> (from 0) the dwellings span <c>y ∈ [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth]</c> and <c>x ∈ [StairWidth, Length]</c>, divided evenly into
/// <see cref="DwellingsPerStorey"/> dwellings; the corridor <c>CO</c> spans <c>[0, Length] × [BaseDepth, BaseDepth + CorridorWidth]</c> and the
/// stair <c>ST</c> <c>[0, StairWidth] × [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth]</c>. Zone IDs per storey: <c>ST</c>, <c>CO</c>, then <c>U1…Un</c> west to east;
/// the floor aggregators prefix them with the storey (<c>L{k}/</c>).
/// </remarks>
public sealed class SteppedBandGenerator : MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<SteppedBandParameters, SteppedBandPresets>
{
    /// <summary>The fewest storeys: one step (ADR-006).</summary>
    public const int MinimumStoreys = 2;

    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SteppedBandGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SteppedBandGenerator);

    /// <summary>Number of dwellings per storey: the band length beside the stair divided by the target width, rounded half away from zero, at least 1.</summary>
    /// <param name="parameters">Parameters.</param>
    /// <returns>Dwellings per storey.</returns>
    public static int DwellingsPerStorey(SteppedBandParameters parameters) =>
        Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round((parameters.Length - parameters.StairWidth) / parameters.UnitWidth, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero));

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(SteppedBandParameters parameters)
    {
        var errors = new List<Diagnostic>();
        if (parameters.StoreyCount < MinimumStoreys)
        {
            errors.Add(Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"{nameof(SteppedBandParameters.StoreyCount)} must be at least {MinimumStoreys} (the band steps back from storey to storey, R001), got {parameters.StoreyCount}."));
        }

        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(SteppedBandParameters.Length), parameters.Length),
            (nameof(SteppedBandParameters.BaseDepth), parameters.BaseDepth),
            (nameof(SteppedBandParameters.StepDepth), parameters.StepDepth),
            (nameof(SteppedBandParameters.CorridorWidth), parameters.CorridorWidth),
            (nameof(SteppedBandParameters.UnitWidth), parameters.UnitWidth),
            (nameof(SteppedBandParameters.StairWidth), parameters.StairWidth),
        };
        Diagnostic[] lengths = values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToArray();
        errors.AddRange(lengths);
        if (lengths.Length > 0)
        {
            return errors;
        }

        if (parameters.Length <= parameters.StairWidth)
        {
            errors.Add(Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"Length {Provenance.Format(parameters.Length)} m must exceed StairWidth {Provenance.Format(parameters.StairWidth)} m, so that the band has dwellings beside the stair (R002)."));
        }

        int steps = parameters.StoreyCount - 1;
        if (parameters.StoreyCount >= MinimumStoreys && parameters.BaseDepth - (steps * parameters.StepDepth) <= 0.0)
        {
            errors.Add(Diagnostic.Error(
                DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter,
                $"BaseDepth {Provenance.Format(parameters.BaseDepth)} m leaves no dwelling depth on the top storey: its {steps} steps of StepDepth "
                + $"{Provenance.Format(parameters.StepDepth)} m take {Provenance.Format(steps * parameters.StepDepth)} m; SYN-TYP-012 needs dwellings on every storey, "
                + "so reduce StoreyCount or StepDepth, or increase BaseDepth."));
        }

        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IReadOnlyList<PlanLayout> Layout(SteppedBandParameters p) =>
        Enumerable.Range(0, p.StoreyCount).Select(k => Storey(p, k)).ToArray();

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(SteppedBandParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StoreyCount), p.StoreyCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.Length), Provenance.Format(p.Length)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.BaseDepth), Provenance.Format(p.BaseDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StepDepth), Provenance.Format(p.StepDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CorridorWidth), Provenance.Format(p.CorridorWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.UnitWidth), Provenance.Format(p.UnitWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.StairWidth), Provenance.Format(p.StairWidth)),
    };

    /// <summary>The plan of storey <paramref name="k"/>: the band stepped back <paramref name="k"/> times from the south.</summary>
    private static PlanLayout Storey(SteppedBandParameters p, int k)
    {
        double south = k * p.StepDepth;
        double north = p.BaseDepth + p.CorridorWidth;
        int count = DwellingsPerStorey(p);
        double width = (p.Length - p.StairWidth) / count;
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone>
        {
            new(new ZoneId("ST"), "Stair", SpaceType.Stair, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, south, p.StairWidth, p.BaseDepth)),
            new(new ZoneId("CO"), "Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor, Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, p.BaseDepth, p.Length, north)),
        };
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            double west = p.StairWidth + (i * width);
            double east = i == count - 1 ? p.Length : p.StairWidth + ((i + 1) * width);
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"U{i + 1}"), $"Unit {i + 1}", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(west, south, east, p.BaseDepth)));
        }

        return new PlanLayout(Polygon2.Rectangle(0.0, south, p.Length, north), zones);
    }
}
```

### Task 5: The stepped band through every simplifier and aggregator

**Files:**
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/SteppedBandTypologyTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SteppedBandGenerator` and its records (Task 4); `Pipeline.Simplifier`, `Pipeline.Aggregator`, `Pipeline.Tolerances`, `PlanTransform.Apply`, `SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings, double)`, `FloorEntry`, `BuildingValidator`, `Totals.Of`, `ToleranceSettings.AreaEquals`.
- Produces: 100 cases, each storey simplified on its own (after the same placement for every storey) and the storeys stacked bottom-up with multiplier 1: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedBand` (32, rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1) m, and rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9) m), `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostStoreys` (32, 2 storeys, and 4 storeys with `BaseDepth` 17 m), and `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheMostStoreysOnARotatedAndTranslatedBand` (16, 4 storeys of 17 m at 131.4°); each validates with no warning, every lower storey keeps a terrace of `Length × StepDepth` facing outdoors, the top roof is the top storey's outline, ground and roof area equal the ground storey's footprint, and no floor is exposed. `PerimeterCoreRejectsATopStoreyNarrowerThanTwiceItsDepth`: 4 storeys of the default 14 m leave a 7 m deep top storey, which *Perimeter Core* (4.57 m) rejects with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087) while storeys 0 to 2 pass, and the other three simplifiers validate with `Stack`. `SemanticMergeGivesEveryStoreyOneDwellingZoneBesideTheStairAndTheCorridor` (dwelling zones of 448, 352, and 256 m²), `SingleZonePerFloorTypeGivesEveryStoreyItsZone` (`E0`, `E1`, `E2` of 576, 468, and 360 m², interzone pairs of 468 and 360 m², no internal mass), and `SingleZoneBuildingHasOnePartPerStoreyOutlineAndTheCoveredSlabsAsInternalMass` (parts of 576, 468, and 360 m², internal mass of 468 m² at 3 m and 360 m² at 6 m, the 36 m walls facing the terraces at y = 3 and 6 m with one window each). No production code changes.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the integration tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/SteppedBandTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.SteppedBand;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.7 stepped band (SYN-TYP-012, ADR-006): one plan per storey (ADR-015), each storey simplified on its own and the storeys stacked bottom-up
/// with multiplier 1, through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed, with the fewest and the most tested storeys. Every lower storey
/// keeps a terrace of Length × StepDepth, an outdoor roof (D-068), and the roofs and terraces together cover the ground storey.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SteppedBandTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/> to every storey alike.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    /// <summary>
    /// The fewest storeys (ADR-006) and the most tested, with the base depth that keeps the top storey wider than twice the perimeter depth: four
    /// storeys of the default 14 m leave a 7 m deep top storey, which <see cref="PerimeterCore"/> rejects (D-087,
    /// <see cref="PerimeterCoreRejectsATopStoreyNarrowerThanTwiceItsDepth"/>).
    /// </summary>
    private static readonly (int Storeys, double BaseDepth)[] Counts = { (SteppedBandGenerator.MinimumStoreys, 14.0), (4, 17.0) };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> CountMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from count in Enumerable.Range(0, Counts.Length)
        select combination.Append(count).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(SteppedBandParameters.Default, null, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedBand(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        AssertValidates(SteppedBandParameters.Default, Placements[placement], simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(CountMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostStoreys(string simplifier, string aggregator, int count)
    {
        AssertValidates(Parameters(count), null, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheMostStoreysOnARotatedAndTranslatedBand(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Parameters(1), Placements[1], simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreRejectsATopStoreyNarrowerThanTwiceItsDepth()
    {
        // Four storeys of the default 14 m: the top storey is 14 − 3 · 3 + 2 = 7 m deep, not wider than 2 × 4.57 m (D-087); the storeys below pass.
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Plans(SteppedBandParameters.Default with { StoreyCount = 4 });

        Result<IFloor>[] floors = plans.Select(p => new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(p)).ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(new[] { true, true, true, false }, floors.Select(f => f.IsSuccess));
        Assert.Contains(floors[3].Diagnostics, d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow);
        Assert.All(new[] { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "SingleZonePerFloor" }, s => AssertValidates(SteppedBandParameters.Default with { StoreyCount = 4 }, null, s, "Stack"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeGivesEveryStoreyOneDwellingZoneBesideTheStairAndTheCorridor()
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Plans(SteppedBandParameters.Default);

        IFloor[] floors = plans.Select(p => new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(p).Value).ToArray();

        Assert.All(floors, f => Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.Corridor, SpaceType.Stair }.OrderBy(t => t), f.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).OrderBy(t => t)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 448.0, 352.0, 256.0 }, floors.Select(f => Math.Round(f.Zones.Single(z => z.SpaceType == SpaceType.DwellingUnit).FloorArea, 9)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZonePerFloorTypeGivesEveryStoreyItsZone()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = Build(SteppedBandParameters.Default, null, "NoSimplification", "SingleZonePerFloorType").Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "E0", "E1", "E2" }, building.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 576.0, 468.0, 360.0 }, building.Zones.Select(z => Math.Round(z.FloorArea, 9)));
        Assert.Empty(building.InternalMasses);
        HorizontalSurface[] interzone = building.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>().Where(h => h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(468.0, interzone.Where(h => h.Zone.Value == "E0" && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling).Sum(h => h.Area), 6);
        Assert.Equal(360.0, interzone.Where(h => h.Zone.Value == "E1" && h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling).Sum(h => h.Area), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SingleZoneBuildingHasOnePartPerStoreyOutlineAndTheCoveredSlabsAsInternalMass()
    {
        IGeneratedBuilding building = Build(SteppedBandParameters.Default, null, "SemanticMerge", "SingleZoneBuilding").Value;

        Zone zone = Assert.Single(building.Zones);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 576.0, 468.0, 360.0 }, zone.Parts.Select(p => Math.Round(p.Footprint.Area, 9)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { (3.0, 468.0), (6.0, 360.0) }, building.InternalMasses.Select(m => (m.Elevation, Math.Round(m.SlabArea, 9))));
        WallSurface[] terraceFacing = building.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Elevation > 0.0 && w.Start.Y == w.End.Y && w.Start.Y < 14.0).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 3.0, 6.0 }, terraceFacing.Select(w => w.Start.Y));
        Assert.All(terraceFacing, w => Assert.Equal(36.0, w.Length, 9));
        Assert.All(terraceFacing, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
    }

    private static SteppedBandParameters Parameters(int count) =>
        SteppedBandParameters.Default with { StoreyCount = Counts[count].Storeys, BaseDepth = Counts[count].BaseDepth };

    private static void AssertValidates(SteppedBandParameters parameters, double[]? placement, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Build(parameters, placement, simplifier, aggregator);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);

        // Every lower storey keeps a terrace of Length × StepDepth; the top storey's roof is its whole outline; together they cover the ground storey.
        IGeneratedBuilding value = building.Value;
        HorizontalSurface[] roofs = value.Surfaces.OfType<HorizontalSurface>()
            .Where(h => h.Kind == HorizontalKind.Ceiling && h.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors)
            .ToArray();
        int n = parameters.StoreyCount;
        double depth = parameters.BaseDepth + parameters.CorridorWidth;
        for (int k = 0; k < n; k++)
        {
            double elevation = (k + 1) * parameters.FloorHeight;
            double expected = k < n - 1 ? parameters.Length * parameters.StepDepth : parameters.Length * (depth - ((n - 1) * parameters.StepDepth));
            double actual = roofs.Where(h => Math.Abs(h.Elevation - elevation) <= Pipeline.Tolerances.Distance).Sum(h => h.Area);
            Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(expected, actual), $"storey {k}: terrace or roof {actual} m², expected {expected} m²");
        }

        Totals totals = Totals.Of(value.Zones, value.Surfaces, value.OrientationDegrees);
        double ground = parameters.Length * depth;
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(ground, totals.GroundArea), $"ground {totals.GroundArea} m², expected {ground} m²");
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(ground, totals.RoofArea), $"roof {totals.RoofArea} m², expected {ground} m²");
        Assert.True(Pipeline.Tolerances.AreaEquals(0.0, totals.ExposedFloorArea), $"exposed floor {totals.ExposedFloorArea} m²");
    }

    private static Result<IGeneratedBuilding> Build(SteppedBandParameters parameters, double[]? placement, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> plans = Plans(parameters);
        var entries = new List<FloorEntry>();
        foreach (IGeneratedPlan storey in plans)
        {
            IGeneratedPlan plan = placement is null ? storey : PlanTransform.Apply(storey, placement[0], placement[1], placement[2]).Value;
            Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
            Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
            entries.Add(new FloorEntry(floor.Value, 1));
        }

        return Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
    }

    private static SteppedBandPresets Presets() =>
        new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    /// <summary>The storeys of a band, bottom-up: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets, or other parameters.</summary>
    private static IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan> Plans(SteppedBandParameters parameters) =>
        new SteppedBandGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(parameters, Presets()).Value;
}
```

## Slice C — `feature/stepped-band-component`

### Task 6: *Stepped Band* component with one plan per storey, and its icon

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (S8.1 file, last changed in S8.6), `scripts/make_icons.py` (S4.1 file, last changed in S8.6)
- Create (generated): `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/SteppedBandComponent.png`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SteppedBandGenerator` and its records (Task 4), `Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>>` (Task 3); the S8.1 base `TypologyGeneratorComponent` (panel *2 Generate*, `AddPreset`, `TryNumbers`, `Preset`, `Output`, `FloorHeightDescription`, `OrientationDescription`); `ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter()`, `ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation`, and `ComponentSupport.Unwrap` (S4.1); `PlanGoo(IGeneratedPlan, Vector3d)`; `RhinoGeometry.UnitScale`; `IconLibrary.For`.
- Produces: `TypologyGeneratorComponent` gains `protected virtual bool PlanPerStorey => false`; when a component overrides it with `true`, its *Plan* output has list access ("The detailed plan of every storey, bottom-up (ADR-015). …"); and `protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result)`, which reports the diagnostics as the single-plan `Output` does and sets the list of `PlanGoo`, storey `k` previewed at the *Preview Location* plus (0, 0, the sum of the floor heights below it × `RhinoGeometry.UnitScale`), while the plans keep elevation 0 (ADR-015). Every other typology component keeps its item output. One component after *Court Cluster* in the file, with its count as an integer input first, its number inputs defaulting to the ADR-006 defaults, *Floor Height*, *Orientation*, one required preset input per space type in the preset record's order (an unconnected one is Grasshopper's missing-input warning, D-065), and *Preview Location* last (D-062):

  | Component (nickname) | Inputs → output | Class | GUID |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | *Stepped Band* (SBand) | Storey Count (SC, 3), Length (L, 36), Base Depth (BD, 14), Step Depth (SD, 3), Corridor Width (CW, 2), Unit Width (UW, 6), Stair Width (SW, 4), Floor Height (FH, 3), Orientation (O, 0), Dwelling Unit (DU), Corridor (Co), Stair (St), Preview Location (PL) → Plan (list) | `SteppedBandComponent` | `3c685bfb-b45b-45af-98d2-8318a6314922` |

  The motif `stepped_band` (a section of three storeys stepping back from the left, each lower roof a terrace, with the rear corridor dark on the right), in the Generate green, mapped after `CourtClusterComponent`. 53 components, 6 parameters, and 60 icons. The seventeen earlier typology components are unchanged.

- [x] **Step 2: Replace `src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.BranchingMall;
using Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;
using Lod.Generators.CareHub;
using Lod.Generators.CourtCluster;
using Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;
using Lod.Generators.FoyerHalls;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.OperatingSuite;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Generators.RadialLobes;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.SteppedBand;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Convert;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;
using Rhino.Geometry;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and output of the typology plan generator components (ADR-006): number inputs with the family's defaults, one required preset input
/// per space type (D-061, D-065), the Preview Location last (D-062), and one Plan output: one plan, or, for a family whose plan differs per storey,
/// the list of storey plans bottom-up (ADR-015).
/// </summary>
public abstract class TypologyGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected const string FloorHeightDescription = "Floor-to-floor height, m.";

    protected const string OrientationDescription = "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.";

    protected const string Metres = " All lengths in metres regardless of model units.";

    protected TypologyGeneratorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description + Metres, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    /// <summary>Whether the Plan output is the list of storey plans of a multi-storey family (ADR-015) rather than one plan.</summary>
    protected virtual bool PlanPerStorey => false;

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        if (PlanPerStorey)
        {
            pManager.AddParameter(
                new PlanParameter(),
                "Plan",
                "Plan",
                "The detailed plan of every storey, bottom-up (ADR-015). Wire it through a plan simplifier into a floor aggregator's Floors, with Multipliers unconnected (one storey each).",
                GH_ParamAccess.list);
        }
        else
        {
            pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Adds the required preset input of one space type.</summary>
    protected static void AddPreset(GH_InputParamManager pManager, string name, string nickname, SpaceType spaceType, string role) =>
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), name, nickname, $"Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}.", GH_ParamAccess.item);

    /// <summary>Reads <paramref name="count"/> number inputs from <paramref name="first"/>; <c>false</c> when one is missing.</summary>
    protected static bool TryNumbers(IGH_DataAccess DA, int first, int count, out double[] values)
    {
        values = new double[count];
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(first + i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads a preset input; <c>null</c> when it is unconnected, which Grasshopper reports as a missing input (D-065).</summary>
    protected static ProgramPreset? Preset(IGH_DataAccess DA, int index)
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo? goo = null;
        return DA.GetData(index, ref goo) && goo is not null ? goo.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the plan with this component's preview location.</summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IGeneratedPlan> result)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the storey plans, bottom-up; each storey's preview is raised to its elevation (the floor
    /// heights below it, in model units) on top of this component's preview location, while the plans keep elevation 0 (ADR-015).
    /// </summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>> result)
    {
        IReadOnlyList<IGeneratedPlan>? plans = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plans is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        Vector3d location = ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA);
        double elevation = 0.0;
        var goos = new List<PlanGoo>();
        foreach (IGeneratedPlan plan in plans)
        {
            goos.Add(new PlanGoo(plan, location + new Vector3d(0.0, 0.0, elevation * RhinoGeometry.UnitScale)));
            elevation += plan.FloorHeight;
        }

        DA.SetDataList(0, goos);
    }
}

public sealed class StairBayBarComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public StairBayBarComponent()
        : base(
            "Stair Bay Bar",
            "SBar",
            "Repeated stair bays in a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006): bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling through the full depth, each stair serving the two dwellings beside it; no corridor. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        StairBayBarParameters d = StairBayBarParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Bay Count", "BC", "Number of stair bays along the bar; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.BayCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Bar depth, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Width", "SW", "Width of each stair along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.StairWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the stairs");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int bays = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref bays) || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 5, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 6) is not { } dwellingUnit || Preset(DA, 7) is not { } stair)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new StairBayBarParameters(bays, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4]);
        Output(DA, new StairBayBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new StairBayBarPresets(dwellingUnit, stair)));
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Build, check Python, and see the icon script fail**

```bash
dotnet build BEMGen.sln -c Release
python -c "import sys, PIL; print(sys.version_info[:2] >= (3, 10), PIL.__version__)"
python scripts/make_icons.py
git status --short
```

Expected: `Build succeeded.`, `0 Warning(s)`, `0 Error(s)`; `True 12.3.0`; the script stops with exit code 1 and the message `Classes without an icon: ['SteppedBandComponent']; icons without a class: []`, and writes nothing (`git status --short` lists only ` M src/Lod.Grasshopper/Components/TypologyGeneratorComponents.cs`). With another Pillow version the script still runs, but the PNG bytes may differ from Step 5's hashes; then Step 6 decides.

- [x] **Step 4: Replace `scripts/make_icons.py` (full file)**

The motif is added after `court_cluster`, and its entry after *Court Cluster* in the Generate group; every other motif and entry is unchanged.

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def office(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # An office block in elevation with a grid of windows.
    box(d, (16, 6, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (18, 38, 58):
        for x in (26, 52):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 16, y + 12), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((40, 76, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def core(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A service core: a box with two lift doors and an up and down arrow.
    box(d, (8, 8, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 34, 42, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((54, 34, 76, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.polygon([(48, 12), (38, 26), (58, 26)], fill=DARK)


def retail(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A shopping bag with its handle.
    d.arc((30, 6, 66, 46), start=180, end=360, fill=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.polygon([(14, 30), (82, 30), (90, 90), (6, 90)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A branching mall in plan: shop blocks (accent) along a T of public routes (white).
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 8, 56, 88), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((8, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE)
    for a, b in [((40, 4), (40, 40)), ((56, 4), (56, 92)), ((40, 56), (40, 92)), ((4, 40), (40, 40)), ((4, 56), (40, 56))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def kitchen(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cooking pot with a lid and two handles.
    box(d, (16, 40, 80, 86), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(4, 50), (16, 50)])
    line(d, [(80, 50), (92, 50)])
    d.rectangle((12, 28, 84, 38), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((42, 16, 54, 28), fill=DARK)


def dining(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    d.ellipse((38, 34, 62, 58), outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(12, 14), (12, 86)], width=6)
    line(d, [(84, 14), (84, 86)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((80, 14, 88, 46), fill=DARK)


def operating_theatre(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

        outline=DARK,
        width=LINE,

def clean_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    d.rectangle((10, 42, 70, 54), fill=WHITE)
    arrow_head(d, (86, 48), "right", 12)

def stair_bay_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    for x in (20, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 30, x + 10, 66), fill=DARK)

def stair_pair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    d.rectangle((42, 26, 54, 70), fill=DARK)


def gallery_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    box(d, (4, 34, 91, 76), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((8, 38, 18, 72), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((77, 38, 87, 72), fill=DARK)
    for x in (36, 54):
        line(d, [(x, 34), (x, 76)], width=6)
    for x in range(6, 90, 16):
        line(d, [(x, 20), (x + 9, 20)], width=6)


def terrace_row(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Attached houses in elevation: three gables sharing their side walls.
    houses = [(6, 34), (34, 62), (62, 90)]
    for left, right in houses:
        middle = (left + right) / 2
        d.polygon([(left, 46), (middle, 22), (right, 46), (right, 88), (left, 88)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for left, right in houses:
        d.rectangle((left + 10, 68, right - 10, 88), fill=DARK)


def point_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A square plate: four dwellings (accent) in a pinwheel around one central core (dark) without façades.
    box(d, (6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((36, 36, 60, 60), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((6, 36), (60, 36)), ((60, 6), (60, 60)), ((36, 60), (90, 60)), ((36, 36), (36, 90))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def winged_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A comb in plan: a band (bottom) with three wings projecting north (up), every wing joined to the band along its root; stairs dark.
    d.polygon(
        [(4, 90), (92, 90), (92, 8), (70, 8), (70, 62), (59, 62), (59, 8), (37, 8), (37, 62), (26, 62), (26, 8), (4, 8)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    for left, right in ((4, 26), (37, 59), (70, 92)):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 30, right - 3, 38), fill=DARK)
    for x in (20, 48, 76):
        d.rectangle((x, 66, x + 6, 87), fill=DARK)


def open_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A U in plan: a north wing (top) across the full width and two side wings, around a court open to the south (bottom); stairs dark.
    d.polygon([(4, 92), (28, 92), (28, 34), (68, 34), (68, 92), (92, 92), (92, 6), (4, 6)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    for x in (24, 45, 66):
        d.rectangle((x, 9, x + 6, 31), fill=DARK)
    for left in (4, 68):
        d.rectangle((left + 3, 58, left + 21, 66), fill=DARK)


def enclosed_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A closed ring in plan: south and north wings across the full width and west and east wings between them, around a court (the hole,
    # transparent) closed on all four sides; stairs dark.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 30, 66, 66), fill=(0, 0, 0, 0), width=6)
    for y in (7, 69):
        for x in (24, 66):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 6, y + 20), fill=DARK)
    for left in (7, 69):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 20, 51), fill=DARK)


def office_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((32, 40, 64, 56), fill=DARK)


def cafeteria(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    for y in (48, 68):

            d.ellipse((x, y, x + 10, y + 10), fill=DARK)


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    box(d, (6, 52, 50, 90), width=6)
    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.arc((18, 20, 70, 72), start=180, end=270, fill=DARK, width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (58, 24), "right", 10)


def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])
        line(d, [(4, k), (91, k)])


def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Cells of one space type merged: two unit rows and a corridor.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 40), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))
    d.rectangle((30, 30, 66, 66), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).

def branching_mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    d.rectangle((18, 62, 78, 70), fill=DARK)

def care_hub(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A communal hub (accent, a table in its middle) with three narrower bedroom wings (accent), each with a corridor (dark) along its axis.
    for xy in [(66, 49, 92, 75), (4, 49, 30, 75), (35, 6, 61, 42)]:
        box(d, xy, fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 42, 66, 82), fill=accent, width=6)

def foyer_halls(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    box(d, (62, 40, 92, 74), fill=accent, width=6)

def operating_suite(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Support rooms (accent) across the west end and bands to their east, south to north: a dirty corridor (dark), a theatre bank (accent), a
    # clean corridor (white), a theatre bank, and a dirty corridor.
    box(d, (4, 6, 92, 90), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(30, 6), (30, 90)], width=6)
    d.rectangle((30, 6, 92, 20), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((30, 76, 92, 90), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((30, 40, 92, 56), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((33, 44, 89, 52), fill=WHITE)


def court_cluster(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Wings around a row of two courts (the holes, transparent): south and north wings across the full width, and a west, middle, and east wing
    # between them; stairs dark.
    box(d, (4, 16, 92, 80), fill=accent, width=6)
    for left in (18, 56):
        box(d, (left, 30, left + 22, 66), fill=(0, 0, 0, 0), width=6)
    for y in (19, 69):
        for x in (24, 46, 68):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 5, y + 8), fill=DARK)
    for left in (7, 43, 81):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 8, 51), fill=DARK)


def stepped_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A section through the band: three storeys stepping back from the south (left), so each lower roof is a terrace; the rear corridor dark.
    for left, top in ((6, 64), (32, 38), (58, 12)):
        box(d, (left, top, 90, top + 26), fill=accent, width=6)
    d.rectangle((76, 12, 90, 90), fill=DARK)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint

    "BemGenAssemblyInfo": (INFO, component(assembly)),

    "OpenCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(open_court)),

    "TransformPlanComponent": (GENERATE, component(transform_plan)),
    "NoSimplificationComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z0)),
    "SemanticMergeComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z1)),
    "PerimeterCoreComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z2)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z3)),
    "StackFloorsComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(stack)),
    "StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(multiplier)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_per_floor_type)),
    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),
    "MapSourceToTargetComponent": (INSPECT, component(map_source_target)),
    "ValidateComponent": (INSPECT, component(validate)),
    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),

    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
icons = sorted(Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons").glob("*.png"))
cell = 24 * 8 + 16
sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (6 * cell, -(-len(icons) // 6) * cell), (235, 235, 235, 255))
for i, path in enumerate(icons):
    big = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").resize((24 * 8, 24 * 8), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    sheet.alpha_composite(big, ((i % 6) * cell + 8, (i // 6) * cell + 8))
Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
sheet.save("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
print(len(icons), "icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
EOF
```

```markdown
- Presets `SteppedBandPresets(DwellingUnit, Corridor, Stair)`. The generator returns one plan per storey (D-095; the interface, how the floor aggregators treat storeys that differ, and the designation of terraces are fixed in the ADR of S8.7). On storey `k` (from 0) the dwellings span `y ∈ [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth]` and `x ∈ [StairWidth, Length]`, divided evenly into `max(1, round((Length − StairWidth) / UnitWidth))` dwellings; the corridor `CO` spans `[0, Length] × [BaseDepth, BaseDepth + CorridorWidth]` on every storey and the stair `ST` `[0, StairWidth] × [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth]`. The part of storey `k − 1`'s roof that storey `k` does not cover, `[0, Length] × [(k − 1)·StepDepth, k·StepDepth]`, is a terrace: an outdoor roof (D-068). Zones per storey `ST`, `CO`, `U1…Un` (west to east).
```

   with

```markdown
- Presets `SteppedBandPresets(DwellingUnit, Corridor, Stair)`. The generator returns one plan per storey ([D-095](decision-log.md#d-095--a-plan-generator-may-produce-a-different-plan-per-storey); the interface, how the floor aggregators treat storeys that differ, and the designation of terraces are fixed in [ADR-015](ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)). On storey `k` (from 0) the dwellings span `y ∈ [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth]` and `x ∈ [StairWidth, Length]`, divided evenly into `max(1, round((Length − StairWidth) / UnitWidth))` dwellings; the corridor `CO` spans `[0, Length] × [BaseDepth, BaseDepth + CorridorWidth]` on every storey and the stair `ST` `[0, StairWidth] × [k·StepDepth, BaseDepth]`. The part of storey `k − 1`'s roof that storey `k` does not cover, `[0, Length] × [(k − 1)·StepDepth, k·StepDepth]`, is a terrace: an outdoor roof (D-068). Zones per storey `ST`, `CO`, `U1…Un` (west to east).
```

```markdown

```

   with

```markdown
- Not modelled: terrace parapets, planting, and shading; an internal gallery instead of a rear corridor; steps on both sides of the band.
- Implemented (S8.7): as specified, as the first `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator` ([ADR-015](ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)): `Generate` returns the storeys bottom-up, each an ordinary plan whose provenance adds `Storey` (from 0); the dwelling count is `SteppedBandGenerator.DwellingsPerStorey`, the same on every storey, so dwelling `Ui` of storey `k + 1` stands on dwelling `Ui` of storey `k`. Zones per storey `ST` (*Stair*), `CO` (*Corridor*), `U1…U5` (*Unit 1…*; five dwellings of 6.4 m by default). By default the three storeys are 576 m², 468 m², and 360 m² (1404 m²), each lower storey keeping a 36 m × 3 m terrace of 108 m²; every storey has 11 windows (33 in all, 201.6 m² of glazing: 70.8, 67.2, and 63.6 m² from the ground up), and the south walls of the stair and the dwellings of storeys 1 and 2 face the terrace below them and keep their windows. Every dwelling and the stair adjoin the corridor over their full width on every storey (6.4 m and 4 m). Validation settles two further essential relationships: `Length` must exceed `StairWidth` (`Length 4 m must exceed StairWidth 4 m, so that the band has dwellings beside the stair (R002).`), and the top storey must keep its dwellings (`BaseDepth 12 m leaves no dwelling depth on the top storey: its 4 steps of StepDepth 3 m take 12 m; SYN-TYP-012 needs dwellings on every storey, so reduce StoreyCount or StepDepth, or increase BaseDepth.`); both are checked only when every length is positive. `StoreyCount` < 2 is `StoreyCount must be at least 2 (the band steps back from storey to storey, R001), got 1.` *Semantic Merge* gives each storey the stair, the corridor, and one dwelling zone (448, 352, and 256 m²); *Perimeter Core* gives every storey `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, and a `CORE` 26.86 m long, and needs the top storey, the narrowest, to be deeper than twice the perimeter depth: with the default 4.57 m the cores of the three default storeys are 6.86 m, 3.86 m, and 0.86 m deep, and four storeys of the default 14 m leave a 7 m deep top storey that fails with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087) while the storeys below pass. The family has no upper bound; 2 to 5 storeys are tested in the generator's tests and 2 to 4 through every simplifier and aggregator.

#### Pipeline consequences of S8.7

Every plan simplifier and floor aggregator was run on the stepped band, each storey simplified on its own and the storeys stacked bottom-up with multiplier 1, canonical, at the two rotated placements of the test suite (every storey placed alike), with 2 and 4 storeys (4 with a 17 m base depth), and with 4 at 131.4°, and, while building the reference, in a sweep of 50 rotations from 0° to 353.5° with translations for the default band, 2 storeys, 4 storeys of 17 m, and a band of other dimensions, and, as a regression, the linear plan, the enclosed court, the court cluster, and the care hub (entries 1/3/1): 6400 combinations without a failure. No step of `Lod.Core` changed apart from the new base class and the helper it shares with `PlanGenerator`, and no tolerance or check was relaxed:

- The floor aggregators already take floors that differ (setbacks since S4) and designate floors and ceilings on the assembled stack (D-068): the part of each lower ceiling that the storey above does not cover faces outdoors and counts as roof, the covered part lies between the storeys. In every method the terraces on each lower storey are `Length × StepDepth`, the roofs and terraces together equal the ground storey's footprint, no floor is exposed, and no storey is unsupported ([ADR-015](ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md), [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md)).
- `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` models every storey, since every entry has multiplier 1; `SingleZonePerFloorType` makes one zone per storey with interzone pairs over the covered slabs; `SingleZoneBuilding` makes one zone with one part per storey outline and turns the covered slabs into internal mass. Their rebuilt walls include the south walls that face the terraces, each with one centred window (D-079).
```

2. Replace

```markdown
The four methods need no rule of their own for a footprint with holes (a court, [ADR-002](ADR-002-geometry.md)). The court lies outside every zone footprint, so the designation on the stack gives it no floor or ceiling piece: the ground and roof areas are the footprint's area without the court, and a storey with the same court as the storey below is fully supported. The storey outlines of the single-zone methods are unions of tiles that keep the hole, one part per outline with an inner ring per court, and their rebuilt walls include the court walls of every storey, which must cover the source court façades (D-041) and get one centred window each with the glazed area they cover (D-079). Every combination of a plan simplifier and a floor aggregator validates for the enclosed court of `HDHM-TYP-005`, canonical and at the two rotated placements of the test suite.

### Heat transfer is a conversion rule (D-069)
```

   with

```markdown
The four methods need no rule of their own for a footprint with holes (a court, [ADR-002](ADR-002-geometry.md)). The court lies outside every zone footprint, so the designation on the stack gives it no floor or ceiling piece: the ground and roof areas are the footprint's area without the court, and a storey with the same court as the storey below is fully supported. The storey outlines of the single-zone methods are unions of tiles that keep the hole, one part per outline with an inner ring per court, and their rebuilt walls include the court walls of every storey, which must cover the source court façades (D-041) and get one centred window each with the glazed area they cover (D-079). Every combination of a plan simplifier and a floor aggregator validates for the enclosed court of `HDHM-TYP-005`, canonical and at the two rotated placements of the test suite.

### Storeys that differ (S8.7)

A generator may return one plan per storey (D-095, [ADR-015](ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)); its storeys are floor entries with multiplier 1, bottom to top, and the four methods need no rule of their own. The designation on the stack makes the part of a lower storey's ceiling that the storey above does not cover an outdoor roof piece (a terrace) and the covered part a piece between the storeys: `Interzone` in `Stack`, `Adiabatic` in `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier`, `Interzone` between `E{k}` and `E{k+1}` in `SingleZonePerFloorType`, which makes one zone per storey, and internal mass in `SingleZoneBuilding`. `Building.RoofArea` counts terraces with the top roof, so for storeys that each stand on the storey below it equals the ground area, and no storey is unsupported. A multiplier above 1 repeats its storey as for any floor and is allowed; `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` then splits every storey with a terrace off as × 1 (D-046). `TerraceTests` (`Lod.Core.Tests`) and the stepped band of `SYN-TYP-012` through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and at the two rotated placements, confirm this; nothing in this ADR changed for it.

### Heat transfer is a conversion rule (D-069)
```

   with

```markdown
- Plans per storey (S8.7, D-095, [ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)): a `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator` returns one ordinary plan per storey, bottom-up, for a family whose plan differs by storey. It checks the parameters and presets once and builds every storey as `PlanGenerator` builds its plan; each plan lies at elevation 0, has the parameters' floor height and orientation, and records its storey index as the provenance parameter `Storey` (from 0). A diagnostic of one storey keeps its code and subject, and its message starts with `Storey k: `.
- Stepped band (`SYN-TYP-012`, S8.7): `StoreyCount` (≥ 2, default 3) storeys of a `Length` 36 m band; on storey `k` the dwellings `U1…Un` (west to east, `n = max(1, round((Length − StairWidth) / UnitWidth))`, five of 6.4 m by default) span from `k · StepDepth` (3 m) to `BaseDepth` (14 m), beside a west stair `ST` (`StairWidth` 4 m) over the same depth, and the corridor `CO` (`CorridorWidth` 2 m) runs along the rear on every storey, adjoined by every dwelling. Each storey stands on the one below, which keeps a terrace of `Length × StepDepth` (108 m² by default), an outdoor roof once stacked (D-068); the walls facing the terraces are outdoor walls with windows. `Length` must exceed `StairWidth`, and `BaseDepth − (StoreyCount − 1) · StepDepth` must be positive (`InvalidParameter`). 576, 468, and 360 m² by default, 11 windows per storey.
- Non-residential families place one zone per department (D-097, [ADR-014](../decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)); their default dimensions are controller design defaults (D-098). The last family of the four-source set, `SYN-TYP-012`, is built in S8.7 as the first generator of one plan per storey ([ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)); with it every family of D-093 except the set-aside `SYN-TYP-013` (D-096) has a generator, eighteen in all.
```
