6. Replace

```markdown
The single-zone methods share one merge (`SingleZoneMerge`); their storey outlines are unions of the zone footprints as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`), so rotated plans give clean outlines whose vertices are the zones' own vertices (S8.2). An outline keeps the footprint's holes, its rebuilt walls include the court walls of every storey, and the court has no floor or ceiling piece, so it adds neither ground nor roof area (S8.3). Programs are aggregated per zone with `EquivalentPropertyAggregator` from the stacked zones (fractions 1), so every invariant is conserved; a zone is conditioned if any source is (D-038). `SingleZoneBuilding` is the former `SingleZoneMerged`, and `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` the former `FloorAreaMultiplier` (D-071).
```

   with

```markdown
The single-zone methods share one merge (`SingleZoneMerge`); their storey outlines are unions of the zone footprints as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`), so rotated plans give clean outlines whose vertices are the zones' own vertices (S8.2). An outline keeps the footprint's holes, its rebuilt walls include the court walls of every storey, and the court has no floor or ceiling piece, so it adds neither ground nor roof area (S8.3). Programs are aggregated per zone with `EquivalentPropertyAggregator` from the stacked zones (fractions 1), so every invariant is conserved; a zone is conditioned if any source is (D-038). `SingleZoneBuilding` is the former `SingleZoneMerged`, and `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` the former `FloorAreaMultiplier` (D-071).

Storeys that differ (S8.7, [ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)): the plans of a multi-storey generator, each simplified on its own, are floor entries with multiplier 1, bottom to top, and every aggregator takes them unchanged. The part of a lower storey's ceiling that the storey above does not cover is an outdoor roof piece (a terrace), the covered part lies between the storeys; `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier` then models every storey, `SingleZonePerFloorType` makes one zone per storey, and `SingleZoneBuilding` one zone with one part per storey outline. `Building.RoofArea` counts the terraces with the top roof. A multiplier above 1 repeats a storey as for any floor and is allowed, but gives another building than the generator's.
```

It moves only that component's viewport preview: the plan, floor, or building data, *Inspect*, *Validate*, and *Convert2BEM* keep the true coordinates, and downstream components do not inherit the move (each wraps its output with its own input). *Convert2BEM* has no such input.

```markdown
- **Typology generators (S8.1, ADR-006).** *Stair Bay Bar*, *Stair Pair*, *Gallery Bar*, and *Terrace Row* follow *Linear Plan Generator*: their number inputs (counts are integers) default to the family's `Default` record, then *Floor Height* (3 m) and *Orientation* (0°), then one required preset input per space type they place (*Dwelling Unit*, and *Stair* except for *Terrace Row*; an unconnected one is Grasshopper's missing-input warning, D-065), then *Preview Location*. Their descriptions name the family and what is not modelled. `WindowOmitted` shows as a remark. *Linear Plan Generator*'s description names `HDHM-TYP-009`; its inputs and GUID are unchanged. *Point Plate*, *Winged Band*, and *Open Court* (S8.2) follow the same pattern with the preset inputs *Dwelling Unit* and *Stair*; on *Point Plate* the *Stair* preset is the core's. *Enclosed Court* (S8.3) follows the same pattern; its plan previews the court as a hole, and every zone, floor, and ceiling Brep of a footprint with holes is one face or closed extrusion with an inner loop per hole (`RhinoGeometry`). *Office Plate* and *Cafeteria* (S8.4) follow the same pattern with the preset inputs *Office* and *Core*, and *Kitchen* and *Dining*; their descriptions name the `SYN-TYP-*` family. The office work area is previewed as a ring around the core. *Radial Lobes*, *Branching Mall*, *Care Hub*, and *Foyer Halls* (S8.5) follow the same pattern, their count input (*Lobe Count*, *Branch Count*, *Wing Count*, *Hall Count*) an integer first, with the preset inputs *Dwelling Unit* and *Stair* (the core's); *Mall* and *Retail*; *Care Bedroom*, *Care Communal*, and *Corridor*; and *Lobby*, *Activity Hall*, and *Service*. *Operating Suite* and *Court Cluster* (S8.6) follow the same pattern, their count input (*Bank Count*, *Court Count*) an integer first, with the preset inputs *Operating Theatre*, *Clean Corridor*, *Dirty Corridor*, and *Clinical Support*, and *Dwelling Unit* and *Stair*; the court cluster's plan previews every court as a hole. *Stepped Band* (S8.7) follows the same pattern, its *Storey Count* an integer first, with the preset inputs *Dwelling Unit*, *Corridor*, and *Stair*; its *Plan* output is a list, one plan per storey bottom-up (ADR-015), and each storey is previewed at its elevation (the floor heights below it) on top of the *Preview Location*, while the plans keep elevation 0.
- **Wiring storeys that differ (S8.7, ADR-015).** Connect the *Stepped Band*'s *Plan* list to a plan simplifier (optionally through *Transform Plan*); Grasshopper runs it once per storey, and its *Floor* output is the list of floors in the same order. Connect that list to a floor aggregator's *Floors* and leave *Multipliers* unconnected: every storey counts once, bottom to top. The simplifiers preview their floors at elevation 0, so the storeys overlap there until they are aggregated. A *Single Zone Building* or *Single Zone per Floor Type* zone whose storey outlines differ is previewed by a boolean union of its storeys, as for setbacks (`RhinoGeometry.MergedZoneBreps`).
```

- [x] **Step 8: Append the S8.7 checklist at the end of `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md`**

Append the content of this code block (not the fence lines around it) after the last line of the file (after the S8.6 result), separated by one blank line, so that it nests under `## Stage checklists` like the S2–S8.6 checklists:

```markdown
### S8.7 checklist — plans per storey and the stepped band (`v0.8.6`)

S8.7 adds one plan generator component in *2 Generate* ([ADR-006](../decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)), the stepped dwelling band with exposed terraces (`SYN-TYP-012`), the first generator that returns one plan per storey ([ADR-015](../decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)). Its *Plan* output is a list; no existing component, input, output, or GUID changes.

#### Toolbar and icons

- [ ] *2 Generate* shows, after *Court Cluster*, *Stepped Band* (a section of three green storeys stepping back from the left, with a dark rear corridor on the right), with its own green icon.
- [ ] *Stepped Band* (SBand) inputs, in order, with their defaults: Storey Count 3, Length 36, Base Depth 14, Step Depth 3, Corridor Width 2, Unit Width 6, Stair Width 4, Floor Height 3, Orientation 0, Dwelling Unit, Corridor, Stair, Preview Location; one output, *Plan*, with list access.
- [ ] Storey Count is an integer. With a preset input unconnected, the generator shows Grasshopper's missing-input warning and outputs nothing (D-065); a *Stair Preset* on the *Corridor* input gives `Error PresetSpaceTypeMismatch [Corridor]: Input Corridor needs a Corridor preset, got 'Example Stair' (Stair).` once, not once per storey.

#### Default plans

1. Place *Stepped Band* with *Dwelling Unit Preset*, *Corridor Preset*, and *Stair Preset* connected; connect *Inspect* to its *Plan*.
   - [ ] No runtime message; *Plan* holds three plans, bottom-up, of 576 m², 468 m², and 360 m²: storey `k` spans x = 0–36 m and y = 3k–16 m, with `ST` at x = 0–4 m, `U1` to `U5` of 6.4 m each, and `CO` at y = 14–16 m over the full length.
   - [ ] The preview shows the three storeys stacked at 0 m, 3 m, and 6 m, each lower storey with a 36 m × 3 m terrace in front of the one above; with *Preview Location* 0, 40, 0 the whole stack moves, and the plans' data stay at elevation 0 (*Inspect*: every zone `z=0`).
   - [ ] 11 windows per storey (33 in all, 201.6 m² of glazing); on storeys 1 and 2 the south walls of `ST` and `U1` to `U5`, which face the terrace below, have windows.
   - [ ] The three *Inspect* reports equal the three sections `storey 0` to `storey 2` of `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/SteppedBand/Snapshots/stepped-band-canonical.txt`, and each *Provenance* shows `SteppedBandGenerator … Storey=k …`.

#### Combinations

2. Connect the *Plan* list to each of the four plan simplifiers (*Perimeter Core* Depth 4.57) and each simplifier's *Floor* list to the four floor aggregators, with *Multipliers* unconnected.
   - [ ] Every simplifier outputs three floors; *Semantic Merge* gives each storey `ST`, `CO`, and one dwelling zone; *Perimeter Core* gives each storey `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West`, and `CORE`.
   - [ ] *Stack Floors* (NoSimplification): the building is 9 m high; *Inspect* shows on storeys 0 and 1 ceiling pieces facing `Outdoors` of 108 m² in all (the terrace: 12 m² of `ST` and 19.2 m² of each dwelling) and the rest `Interzone` with the storey above; no `UnsupportedStorey` warning.
   - [ ] *Single Zone per Floor Type*: zones `E0`, `E1`, `E2`; *Single Zone Building*: one zone `BUILDING` previewed as one stepped solid, (0, 0, 0)–(36, 16, 9), with its windows.
   - [ ] For all 16 combinations, *Validate* gives Passed = True (only `ConditionedFloorArea` may read `note`), `Building.RoofArea` reads 576, no aggregator shows a warning, and *Convert2BEM* with Override = False converts without an error; every zone Brep has a positive volume (D-091).

#### Counts and rotated placement

3. Set Storey Count to 2, and to 4 with Base Depth 17, and repeat step 2 for each.
   - [ ] Two and four plans; *Validate* gives Passed = True for every combination.
   - [ ] Storey Count 4 with Base Depth 14 through *Perimeter Core*: the top storey (7 m deep) gives `Error PlanTooNarrow: …` and no floor for it; the other three storeys give floors.
4. Put the *Plan* list through *Transform Plan* with a 30° rotation and a move by 5.3, −2.1, 0 (every storey alike), then through each simplifier and aggregator; repeat with 131.4° and 15.08, −153.9, 0, and with Storey Count 4 and Base Depth 17 at 131.4°.
   - [ ] *Validate* gives Passed = True for all 16 combinations of each placement, with the same total glazing as the unrotated band.

#### Parameters

5. Change inputs one at a time and set each back afterwards.
   - [ ] Storey Count 1: `Error InvalidParameter: StoreyCount must be at least 2 (the band steps back from storey to storey, R001), got 1.`
   - [ ] Storey Count 5 with Base Depth 12: `Error InvalidParameter: BaseDepth 12 m leaves no dwelling depth on the top storey: its 4 steps of StepDepth 3 m take 12 m; SYN-TYP-012 needs dwellings on every storey, so reduce StoreyCount or StepDepth, or increase BaseDepth.`
   - [ ] Length 4: `Error InvalidParameter: Length 4 m must exceed StairWidth 4 m, so that the band has dwellings beside the stair (R002).`; Step Depth 0: `Error InvalidParameter: StepDepth must be positive, got 0.`
   - [ ] Corridor Width 0.8: six `WindowOmitted` remarks, two per storey, each starting `Storey k: Outdoor wall CO/…`.
```

- [x] **Step 9: Check the code fences of the edited documents**

Check the code fences of the documents of Tasks 2 and 7: every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, and no file ends inside a block.

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path

fence = "`" * 3
names = [
    "docs/architecture/domain-model.md",
    "docs/architecture/pipeline.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md",
    "docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md",
    "docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md",
    "docs/research/program-presets.md",
]
for name in names:
    lines = Path(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")
    inside, blocks, problems = False, 0, []
    for number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
        mark = line.strip()
        if not mark.startswith(fence):
            continue
        if not inside and mark == fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block without a language")
        if inside and mark != fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block inside a block")
        blocks += inside
        inside = not inside
    if inside:
        problems.append("the file ends inside a block")
    print(f"{name}: {blocks} code blocks, " + ("; ".join(problems) or "fences balanced"))
EOF
```

docs/architecture/pipeline.md: 1 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md: 2 code blocks, fences balanced
docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md: 0 code blocks, fences balanced

Checkpoint 1 (D-030) is reached: stages S0–S4 of the [implementation roadmap](docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) are complete (tag `v0.4.0`), and so are the revision stages S4.1 (tag `v0.4.1`, D-064) and S4.2 (tag `v0.4.2`, D-073), stage S5 (tag `v0.5.0`, D-079), and sub-stages S8.1 (tag `v0.8.0`), S8.2 (tag `v0.8.1`), S8.3 (tag `v0.8.2`, D-084), S8.4 (tag `v0.8.3`, D-101), S8.5 (tag `v0.8.4`, D-103), S8.6 (tag `v0.8.5`, D-105), and S8.7 (tag `v0.8.6`, D-107): S8 is complete, with a plan generator for each of the 18 families of the four-source set except the set-aside `SYN-TYP-013` (D-096). The pipeline runs end to end for eighteen plan generators: program presets (the residential `ExampleResidentialPresets` and, for the non-residential departments of [ADR-014](docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md), `ExampleNonResidentialPresets`, one typed preset and default preset component per space type) → a plan generator (one `PlanGenerator` subclass per typology family with typed parameter and preset records, [ADR-006](docs/decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md); `SYN-TYP-001` to `009` are the *High-Density Housing* families `HDHM-TYP-001` to `009`, D-099: `LinearPlanGenerator`, the double-sided internal corridor bar `SYN-TYP-009`; the bar families `StairBayBarGenerator` 001, `StairPairGenerator` 008, `GalleryBarGenerator` 002 with its gallery not modelled, and `TerraceRowGenerator` 006 with one dwelling zone per plot and storey, D-085; the non-rectangular families `PointPlateGenerator` 007, a square plate whose core has no outdoor wall, `WingedBandGenerator` 003, a comb, and `OpenCourtGenerator` 004, a U; `EnclosedCourtGenerator` 005, four wings around a court closed on all four sides, the first footprint with a hole; and the non-residential `OfficePlateGenerator` `SYN-TYP-017`, whose office work area is one ring-shaped zone around a central `Core` zone, `CafeteriaGenerator` `SYN-TYP-016`, a `Kitchen` zone along the whole north edge of a larger `Dining` zone; the hub-and-arms families on the shared `HubArmsLayout`, a square hub with up to four arms (D-100, D-102): `RadialLobesGenerator` 010, `BranchingMallGenerator` 014, `CareHubGenerator` 018, and `FoyerHallsGenerator` 019; the operating suite `OperatingSuiteGenerator` 015, theatre banks between alternating clean and dirty corridors beside the support rooms, and the court cluster `CourtClusterGenerator` 011, stair-bay wings around a row of courts, the first footprint with several holes (D-104); and `SteppedBandGenerator` (`SYN-TYP-012`), the first `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator`, which returns one plan per storey, bottom-up; each storey is simplified on its own and stacked by any floor aggregator with multiplier 1, and the part of a lower roof that the storey above leaves free is a terrace, an outdoor roof designated on the assembled stack (D-068, [ADR-015](docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)); the non-residential ones zoned one zone per department, D-097; one typed preset per space type, D-061; optionally rotated and moved by `PlanTransform`, D-070) → plan simplifiers (`NoSimplification`, `SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`) → floor aggregators (`Stack`, `StackedFloorZoneMultiplier`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`, D-071; floors and ceilings designated on the assembled stack, D-068) → validation → `Convert2BEM` (heat transfer between zones an export option, off by default, D-069), as described in [docs/architecture/pipeline.md](docs/architecture/pipeline.md). Every generator places one centred window per outdoor wall (D-026), except on a wall shorter than 1 m or where the window would be narrower or lower than 0.3 m (`GeometryLimits`, info `WindowOmitted`, [ADR-011](docs/decisions/ADR-011-minimum-wall-and-window-geometry.md)). Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt (`SemanticMerge`, `PerimeterCore`, `SingleZonePerFloor`, `SingleZonePerFloorType`, `SingleZoneBuilding`), every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the source windows it covers (`CentredWindows`, D-079, [ADR-012](docs/decisions/ADR-012-centred-windows.md)); the other steps keep their input's windows. Merged zones are unions of their sources as tiles that keep the sources' own vertices, so they may have holes and stay exact on rotated plans, and vertices on edges are recognised at their original positions (D-088, [ADR-002](docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md)). Every step supports footprints with holes: a court is outside the building, its edges are outdoor walls facing into it, every footprint edge, court edges included, must be covered by outdoor zone edges (ADR-002), and `PerimeterCore` gives the court façades their own zones `P-Court-<Bin>` around a core that is a ring, several of them in the court cluster, where *Perimeter Core* gives each court its own `P-Court-<Bin>-k` zones ([ADR-008](docs/decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)); `PerimeterCore` needs every part of a footprint, including the wings between the outer and the court façades, to be wider than twice its depth and otherwise fails with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087). Its perimeter zones keep the footprint's own vertices, so short façades stay covered on rotated plans (ADR-008, S8.5). Vertical aggregation follows [ADR-013](docs/decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md), which supersedes [ADR-009](docs/decisions/ADR-009-vertical-aggregation.md) where it differs. In *Convert2BEM* and the previews, zone solids are outward-oriented and every surface and window faces out of its zone (D-091). ADR-006, ADR-011, ADR-012, ADR-013, ADR-014, ADR-015, and the S4.2, S5, S8.1, S8.2, S8.3, S8.4, S8.5, S8.6, and S8.7 plans were accepted by the controller and await the owner's reading (D-075, D-080, D-084, D-098). In Grasshopper every component has an icon drawn by `scripts/make_icons.py` (D-063), and the generator, *Transform Plan*, simplifier, and aggregator components have a *Preview Location* input that moves only their preview (D-062). Until the owner says otherwise, changes need not keep backward compatibility, but component GUIDs never change (D-064).

The precedent set is the four-source family set `SYN-TYP-001` to `SYN-TYP-019` of every program type, housing and non-residential ([docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md](docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md), D-093, D-094); `SYN-TYP-013` is set aside (D-096), non-residential floors are zoned one zone per department (D-097, [ADR-014](docs/decisions/ADR-014-program-types-and-department-zoning.md)), and BEMGen documents use the `SYN-TYP-*` IDs (D-099). The families `017` and `016` were built in S8.4, `010`, `014`, `018`, `019` in S8.5, the hub-and-arms families with at most four arms because the shared layout is axis-aligned (D-100), `015` and `011` in S8.6, and `012` in S8.7, with one plan per storey (D-095, D-106): all nine new families are built (D-098).

Not implemented yet: `Lod.Export` and Convert2IDF (S6, next), and Grasshopper UX and release (S9). The precedent study of S7 was carried out in a separate repository (D-082); its nine families are paraphrased in [docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md](docs/research/precedents/hdhm-typologies.md). A zone only about 1 m wide on a rotated plan can fail validation after a merging simplifier, a known limit of clipping on the grid (D-090). The example program presets hold illustrative values, not DOE prototype values, and the `Convert2BEM` output layout stays provisional until a ClimateStudio reference definition exists (D-023).
```

```markdown
Checkpoint 1 reached: stages S0–S4 of the [implementation roadmap](docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md) are complete (tag `v0.4.0`), followed by the revision stages S4.1 (tag `v0.4.1`) and S4.2 (tag `v0.4.2`), stage S5 (tag `v0.5.0`), and sub-stages S8.1 (tag `v0.8.0`), S8.2 (tag `v0.8.1`), S8.3 (tag `v0.8.2`), S8.4 (tag `v0.8.3`), S8.5 (tag `v0.8.4`), S8.6 (tag `v0.8.5`), and S8.7 (tag `v0.8.6`), which completes S8. In Rhino 8 the plugin runs the whole pipeline for eighteen plan generators: program presets with conditioning (a default preset component per space type, housing and non-residential); the linear plan generator and the eight other typologies of the *High-Density Housing* precedent study (`SYN-TYP-001`–`009`, ADR-006), the bars *Stair Bay Bar*, *Stair Pair*, *Gallery Bar*, and *Terrace Row*, the non-rectangular *Point Plate*, *Winged Band*, and *Open Court*, *Enclosed Court*, whose footprint has the court as a hole, the non-residential *Office Plate* (`SYN-TYP-017`, a work-area ring around a central core) and *Cafeteria* (`SYN-TYP-016`, a kitchen and servery edge beside a dining field), and the branching plans on one hub-and-arms layout, *Radial Lobes* (`SYN-TYP-010`), *Branching Mall* (`SYN-TYP-014`), *Care Hub* (`SYN-TYP-018`), *Foyer Halls* (`SYN-TYP-019`), the operating suite *Operating Suite* (`SYN-TYP-015`, theatre banks between separate clean and dirty corridors), *Court Cluster* (`SYN-TYP-011`, wings around a row of courts, one hole per court), and *Stepped Band* (`SYN-TYP-012`, a dwelling band that steps back storey by storey so that each lower roof keeps a terrace, the first generator with one plan per storey, [ADR-015](docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md)), the non-residential ones zoned one zone per department (D-097, ADR-014), each with one preset input per space type and explicit windows (none on a wall shorter than 1 m or for a window below 0.3 m, ADR-011); *Transform Plan* to rotate and move a plan; four plan simplifiers (Z0–Z3); four floor aggregators (Stack, StackedFloorZoneMultiplier, SingleZonePerFloorType, SingleZoneBuilding) that designate floors and ceilings on the assembled stack and warn about unsupported storeys; invariant validation; and `Convert2BEM`, which writes heat transfer between zones only when asked (off by default). Wherever zones are merged and walls are rebuilt, every outdoor wall carries one centred window with the glazed area of the windows it covers, so glazing is conserved per wall, façade, orientation, and building (D-079); merged zones stay exact on rotated plans (D-088); every simplifier and aggregator supports footprints with one or more holes, with the court façades as outdoor walls; and zone solids, surfaces, and windows are oriented out of their zones (D-091). Every component has an icon, and every component with a viewport preview has a *Preview Location* input that moves only its preview. The pipeline is described in [docs/architecture/pipeline.md](docs/architecture/pipeline.md); the example definition `examples/grasshopper/checkpoint-1-pipeline.gh`, which wires every simplifier into every aggregator, is still to be built by a person. Next: Convert2IDF (S6), then Grasshopper UX and release (S9). The `Convert2BEM` output stays provisional until a ClimateStudio reference definition exists (D-023), and the example program presets hold illustrative values, not DOE prototype values.
```

- Passed: *Stepped Band* (SBand) in *2 Generate*, primary, with the GUID of the S8.7 plan and a 24 × 24 icon; the integer Storey Count first, the presets in record order (Dwelling Unit, Corridor, Stair), *Preview Location* last; unconnected presets give Grasshopper's missing-input warnings.

- Passed: the terraces (*Single Zone per Floor* → *Stack Floors*): the ground storey's ceiling has a piece between storeys over y = 3–16 m (Adiabatic, +z) and an outdoor terrace piece over y = 0–3 m (Outdoors, +z); the middle storey likewise (y = 6–16 m between storeys, y = 3–6 m terrace); the top ceiling is a roof (+z); the floors above the ground face −z (Adiabatic), the ground floor −z (Ground).
- Passed: the 42 zone Breps inspected are valid solids, with no negative volume anywhere; *Single Zone Building* (1, 1, 1) previews one solid (0, 0, 0)–(36, 16, 9) with three parts, *Validate* Passed = `True`, `Building.RoofArea` 576.
- Passed: all 80 combinations (the band on the canonical plan, rotated by 30° and moved by (5.3, −2.1, 0), rotated by 131.4° and moved by (15.08, −153.9, 0), and with 2 storeys and with 4 storeys of Base Depth 17 m at 131.4°, each through the four simplifiers and the four aggregators with multiplier 1 per storey): *Validate* Passed = `True`, no error, `Building.RoofArea` passed.

- Not performed, open for a person (D-058): the viewport look of the previews, the icons in the toolbar, the *Inspect* reports compared with the snapshot (the generator tests check them), and example definitions.

The log ends with D-105 when this plan is approved, so the entries are D-106 and D-107; if the log ends with another number, use the next free numbers, change the references to them (D-107 cites D-106; Tasks 4 and 9 of this plan cite D-106 for the check `Length > StairWidth`; ADR-015's Decisions line of Step 8 and AGENTS.md of Step 5 cite D-106; AGENTS.md cites D-107; the roadmap Progress line of Step 6 cites D-106) to match, and say so in the commit body.

```markdown
### D-106 — Plans per storey: a sibling generator base, list wiring, and multipliers

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-098) · **Status:** Provisional
- Why: D-095 left the interface, the treatment of storeys that differ, and the designation of terraces to an ADR; [ADR-015](ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md) records the options and this decision.
- A family whose plan differs per storey is a `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator<TParameters, TPresets>` (`Lod.Core.Plans`) returning one ordinary `IGeneratedPlan` per storey, bottom-up, sharing the preset checks, surfaces, windows, and provenance with `PlanGenerator` through one internal helper (`PlanBuilder`), so the plans of the sixteen single-plan families are unchanged; each plan records `Storey` (from 0), and a storey's diagnostics are prefixed `Storey k: `. Rejected: every generator returning a list, a storey index parameter, and a new pipeline type for a stack of storeys.
- No floor aggregator, validation rule, or pipeline type changes: the storeys are floor entries with multiplier 1, and the designation on the assembled stack (D-068) already makes the uncovered part of a lower ceiling an outdoor roof (a terrace) and the covered part a piece between storeys, with ground and roof areas that validate (`TerraceTests`, which passed on the S8.6 code). Multipliers above 1 are allowed and repeat a storey; the generator's building is the one with every multiplier 1.
- In Grasshopper the generator's *Plan* output is a list, wired item-wise through a simplifier into an aggregator's *Floors* with *Multipliers* unconnected; each storey's preview is raised to its elevation, while the plans stay at elevation 0.
- `SteppedBandGenerator` additionally requires `Length > StairWidth` (a dwelling row of no width otherwise), a check ADR-006 did not list; its *Implemented (S8.7)* note records it.
- With four storeys of the default 14 m base depth the top storey is 7 m deep and fails *Perimeter Core* with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087); the integration tests therefore run four storeys with a 17 m base depth and test the default-depth case as that failure, and no default changed. The core of the default band's top storey under *Perimeter Core* is 0.86 m deep, below the about 1 m of D-090, yet every combination validates, at both rotated placements and in a sweep of 50 rotations; D-090 stays the known limit.
```

```markdown
### D-107 — S8.7 complete; S8 complete

- **Date:** <date> · **Decided by:** controller (unsupervised run, D-098) · **Status:** Provisional
- Sub-stage S8.7 (D-098) is complete and tagged `v0.8.6`; after its gate and a fresh clone of the tag pass `scripts/verify.ps1`, `main` and `v0.8.6` are pushed (D-098). [ADR-015](ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md), accepted by the controller under D-098 and pending the owner's reading, fixes plans per storey (D-095, D-106).
- New generator and component in *2 Generate*, with icon: *Stepped Band* (`SteppedBandGenerator`, `SYN-TYP-012`, SBand, `3c685bfb-b45b-45af-98d2-8318a6314922`): a band of a west stair, dwellings, and a rear corridor that every dwelling adjoins, stepping back `StepDepth` per storey so that each lower roof keeps a terrace of `Length × StepDepth`; by default 3 storeys of 576, 468, and 360 m², 33 windows. Its *Plan* output is the list of storeys, bottom-up.
- No step of `Lod.Core` changed apart from the new base and its shared helper; no tolerance or check was relaxed. The twenty-four existing snapshots are byte-identical; one canonical snapshot is new. 210 core, 463 generator, and 1485 integration tests pass on `net8.0`, including the band with every simplifier and aggregator on the canonical plans, at the two rotated placements, with 2 and 4 storeys, and with 4 at 131.4°; a sweep of 50 rotations for four bands and, as a regression, the linear plan, the enclosed court, the court cluster, and the care hub found no failure in 6400 combinations.
- Smoke test (D-056): the controller's scripted headless Grasshopper session on the verified reference (`a98d4c5`, whose `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` equal the executed branch) in Rhino 8.25.25314.11001 (.NET 8.0.31) passed every performed item: the component registered with its GUID, the integer Storey Count first, the presets in record order, *Preview Location* last, and a 24 × 24 icon; the *Plan* list of three storeys of 576, 468, and 360 m², each previewed at its elevation; every simplifier returning three floors and *Stack Floors* validating with multipliers 1 and with its *Multipliers* left at the default, roof and ground area 576 m²; the terraces facing outdoors and the covered parts between storeys; all 42 zone Breps inspected valid solids and no negative volume anywhere; all 80 combinations (five placements and counts, four simplifiers, four aggregators) with *Validate* passing; and the error cases. It is recorded in `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` and the close-out commit; the viewport look, the icons in the toolbar, and example definitions stay open for a person (D-058).
- With S8.7, S8 is complete: 18 plan generators, one for every family of the four-source set except the set-aside `SYN-TYP-013` (D-096). S6 (Convert2IDF) can start; S9 follows S6.
```

- [x] **Step 10: Check the code fences of every document of the stage**

Check the code fences of every Markdown file this stage touches, this plan included, and the entries and sections appended to the decision log and the smoke-test guide: every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, and no file ends inside a block.

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
import re
from pathlib import Path

fence = "`" * 3
names = [
    "AGENTS.md",
    "README.md",
    "docs/architecture/domain-model.md",
    "docs/architecture/pipeline.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md",
    "docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md",
    "docs/decisions/decision-log.md",
    "docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md",
    "docs/plans/2026-09-30-implementation-roadmap.md",
    "docs/plans/2026-10-02-s8.7-per-storey-plans-stepped-band.md",
    "docs/research/precedents/syn-typologies.md",
    "docs/research/program-presets.md",
]
for name in names:
    lines = Path(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("\n")
    inside, blocks, problems = False, 0, []
    for number, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
        mark = line.strip()
        if not mark.startswith(fence):
            continue
        if not inside and mark == fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block without a language")
        if inside and mark != fence:
            problems.append(f"line {number} opens a block inside a block")
        blocks += inside
        inside = not inside
    if inside:
        problems.append("the file ends inside a block")
    print(f"{name}: {blocks} code blocks, " + ("; ".join(problems) or "fences balanced"))
log = Path("docs/decisions/decision-log.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
guide = Path("docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
entries = [part for part in log.split("\n### ")[1:] if part.startswith("D-10")]
sections = [part for part in re.split(r"\n(?=#{3,4} )", guide) if re.match(r"#{3,4} .*S8\.[67]", part)]
for kind, parts in (("decision-log entries D-100 to D-107", entries), ("smoke-test sections of S8.6 and S8.7", sections)):
    ending = [part.split("\n")[0] for part in parts if part.rstrip().split("\n")[-1].strip().startswith(fence)]
    print(f"{len(parts)} {kind}, ending with a fence line: " + (", ".join(ending) or "none"))
EOF
```

## Exit criteria

- Every floor aggregator designates the uncovered part of a lower storey's ceiling as an outdoor roof (a terrace) and the covered part as lying between the storeys, with ground and roof areas that validate (`TerraceTests`, passing on the code of S8.6); no aggregation or validation rule changed.
- ADR-015 decides plans per storey and cites D-106; ADR-006 and ADR-013 refer to it.
- `MultiStoreyPlanGenerator` returns one plan per storey, bottom-up, with the provenance key `Storey` and storey-prefixed diagnostics; its tests failed to build on the code before it; `PlanGenerator` shares its rules through `PlanBuilder` and every existing output is unchanged.
- `SteppedBandGenerator` (012) implements ADR-006 with its defaults, zone IDs and order, and validation (at least 2 storeys, no upper bound, the top storey keeps its dwellings, and `Length > StairWidth`); its tiling, adjacency, stepping, window, invalid-parameter, preset, determinism, provenance, and snapshot tests failed to build on the code before it.
- The band validates with every plan simplifier and floor aggregator, on its canonical plans, at two rotated and moved placements, with 2 and 4 storeys, and with 4 at 131.4° (100 cases), each lower storey keeping a terrace of `Length × StepDepth` and the roofs covering the ground storey; a 7 m top storey fails *Perimeter Core* with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087).
- One generator component in *2 Generate*, with the GUID of this plan, its defaults, an integer Storey Count, required preset inputs, *Preview Location* last, a *Plan* list previewed per storey at its elevation, and an icon (60 icons, the existing ones byte-identical); no existing component, input, output, or GUID changed.
- Snapshots: the twenty-four existing snapshots byte-identical; one new canonical snapshot.
- ADR-006, ADR-013, `pipeline.md`, `domain-model.md`, `syn-typologies.md`, and `program-presets.md` describe S8.7; the S8.7 smoke-test checklist and its headless result are in the guide.
- Every Markdown file the stage touches has balanced code fences.
- `scripts/verify.ps1` prints `VERIFY PASSED`; tests: 210 core, 463 generators, 1485 integration on `net8.0`; 0 warnings.
- Version 0.8.6, tag `v0.8.6`; AGENTS.md, README, the roadmap Progress line and detailed-plans row, and the decision-log entries D-106 and D-107 are written; this plan is ticked and marked Done.
- After a fresh clone of `v0.8.6` passes `scripts/verify.ps1`, `main` and `v0.8.6` are pushed without force (D-098). S8 is complete; S6 follows.

## Notes for the reviewer

1. **Source.** Every code block, snapshot, and documentation replacement of Tasks 1 to 7 was extracted programmatically from a verified reference implementation built on `main` at `a4db33e`, 7 commits from `1d286da` to `a98d4c5`, each passing `scripts/verify.ps1` with 0 warnings; the tasks keep the reference's commit order, one task per commit (no reordering was needed: a relative-link check of every reference commit found no link to a file that a later commit creates; ADR-015 is created in Task 2, and the first links to it come in Task 7). The only content not taken from the reference is this plan's own commit, the fence checks (Task 7, Step 9; Task 9, Step 10), and the close-out of Task 9 (adapted from the reference's proposed texts, with ADR-015's citation of D-106 added there because the reference could not cite an entry that did not exist yet). Every task was then replayed on a worktree of `main` at `a4db33e` (its branch standing in for `main`; nothing fetched, pulled, tagged, or pushed), taking each file content, each replacement, and each script from this plan's text: the passing characterisation tests of Task 1, the red build errors of Tasks 3 and 4 (with line and column numbers and in the compiler's order), the single failing snapshot test of Task 4 and the received snapshot (byte-identical to the expected content), the unchanged snapshots after Task 3, the integration matrix of Task 5, the failure of `make_icons.py` before Task 6 and the 60 hashes after it (every PNG byte-identical to the reference's), the counts after every task, a 0-warning build after every task, every documentation replacement (each "Replace" text occurs exactly once when it is applied), the fence checks, the link checks, the close-out edits, and `VERIFY PASSED` on every merge are observed results. The replay's end state equals the reference: `git diff --stat ref/s8.7 replay/s8.7` limited to the 20 paths the reference changes prints nothing for the state before the close-out, and after it lists only `docs/decisions/ADR-015-plans-per-storey.md` (the citation of D-106, Task 9, Step 8) and `docs/development/grasshopper-smoke-test.md` (the result section of Task 9, Step 7); `Directory.Build.props` (0.8.6), AGENTS.md, README.md, the decision log, the roadmap, and this plan are files the reference does not change. Every observation of a first replay was observed again, unchanged, in a second replay of the final plan from a fresh worktree.
2. **Slices and branches.** The roadmap names no slices for S8.7; this plan uses four slices and a close-out, named after D-004: `feature/per-storey-plans` (Tasks 1–3), `feature/stepped-band` (Tasks 4–5), `feature/stepped-band-component` (Task 6), `docs/architecture-per-storey-plans-stepped-band` (Tasks 7–8), and `chore/repo-s8.7-close-out`. ADR-015 is committed in the first slice, before the base class it decides. Merges rebase onto local `main`. As in S8.4 to S8.6, the stage is pushed at its end (D-098): Task 10 is the controller's, after the tag and a fresh clone of it pass `scripts/verify.ps1`. `origin/main` was at `a4db33e` when this plan was written, so the push is a fast-forward.
3. **Links.** A relative-link check (file targets and heading anchors, GitHub's slug rule) over every Markdown file of the replay found: no broken link at the plan commit or after any commit of the replay (checked after Tasks 3, 5, 7, 8, and 9). Task 7 renames the heading `## Grasshopper components through S8.6` of `pipeline.md` to `… through S8.7` and updates its two links (in `pipeline.md` and `program-presets.md`) in the same commit.
4. **The baseline is 193 core, 427 generators, and 1385 integration tests** (D-105).
5. **Tests that were not test-first, and why.** The characterisation tests of Task 1 (11) passed at once in the reference and in the replay: they confirm that the designation on the assembled stack (D-068, since S4.2) already makes the terraces of storeys that step back outdoor roofs and their covered parts pieces between storeys, with every aggregator, and that ground and roof areas validate; this is the finding of ADR-015 that no aggregation or validation rule needs a change, so there was nothing to fix test-first in `Lod.Core`'s aggregation. The integration matrix of Task 5 (100 cases) passed at once for the same reason. Each of those tasks says to stop if a case fails. Task 3's move of `PlanGenerator`'s body into `PlanBuilder` is a refactoring, checked by every existing generator test and the twenty-four snapshots. Task 6 is Grasshopper code, checked by the build, the embedded icons, and Task 8. The build-error red phases list only declaration-level errors: the compiler stops before binding method bodies.
6. **Choices of the reference (D-106).** A sibling base class rather than a list from every generator, a storey index parameter, or a new pipeline type (ADR-015, options 1 to 4); shared rules through one internal helper, so a test proves that storey 0 equals the plan of a `PlanGenerator` of the same layout, provenance aside from `Storey=0`; parameter and preset errors once, before any layout; storey diagnostics prefixed `Storey {k}: ` with their subject kept as the ID within the storey's plan, not `L{k}/…`; multipliers above 1 allowed. The stepped band follows ADR-006 and adds one validation ADR-006 does not list: `Length > StairWidth`, without which the dwelling row would have no width (the linear plan has the same check); both it and the top-storey check run only when every length is positive, so each invalid input gives exactly one error.
7. **Four storeys and *Perimeter Core*.** Four storeys of the default 14 m base depth leave a 7 m deep top storey, which *Perimeter Core* (4.57 m) rejects with `PlanTooNarrow` (D-087); the integration tests therefore run four storeys with `BaseDepth` 17 m through every combination and test the default-depth case as that expected failure (storeys 0 to 2 pass, storey 3 fails). No default changed. The core of the default band's top storey under *Perimeter Core* is 26.86 m × 0.86 m, below the "about 1 m" of D-090, yet every combination validates, at both rotated placements and in the sweep of note 8 (D-106).
8. **Sweeps.** An uncommitted sweep in the reference (50 rotations from 0° to 353.5° with translations, every storey placed alike, each through the 4 simplifiers × 4 aggregators, 800 runs per configuration) found no failure for the stepped band with 3 storeys, 2 storeys, 4 storeys of `BaseDepth` 17 m, and 4 storeys with other dimensions, and, as a regression sweep, the linear plan, the enclosed court, the court cluster, and the care hub (1, 3, 1): 8 configurations, 6400 combinations. The sweep was not replayed.
9. **Smoke-test values.** The values of Task 8 were observed in one scripted headless Rhino 8.25 session (D-056) on the reference's last commit `a98d4c5`, which reported the informational version `0.8.5+a98d4c5…`; they were not re-observed on the replay. Task 8, Step 1 checks that `src/`, `tests/`, and `scripts/` of the branch equal the reference's, so the record applies; the replay found no difference. The viewport, the toolbar icons, the *Inspect* texts beyond the footprint line, the zone IDs, the *Validate* glazing values, a wrong preset on one input, the `WindowOmitted` remarks with a narrow corridor, and *Convert2BEM* with Override = False were not observed.
10. **Icons.** The 59 existing PNGs are byte-identical after Task 6 (Pillow 12.3.0); the new one equals the reference's bytes. The contact-sheet description is from the replay's sheet.
11. **Code fences.** The S8.6 close-out on `main` (`a4db33e`) left no stray fence line: an independent check of every Markdown file it touched finds none, so this plan repairs nothing. It still says, wherever text is appended, that a block's content is appended without its fence lines, and checks every Markdown file of the stage, this plan included, after the documentation commit and before the close-out commit (Task 7, Step 9; Task 9, Step 10): every opening fence line has a language, every closing one is bare, no file ends inside a block, and no decision-log entry and no appended smoke-test section ends with a fence line. In the replay, the checks of Task 7, Step 9 and Task 9, Step 10 printed the expected output; an independent check written apart from the plan's script found no problem in the thirteen files after the close-out merge, nor on `main` at `a4db33e` in the six Markdown files the S8.6 close-out touched.
12. **Clean working tree.** The `git status` expectations assume a working tree with no untracked files outside `.gitignore` before Task 1.
13. **D-numbers.** The reference's report proposed D-106 for the choices of notes 6 and 7, which Tasks 4 and 9 cite, and D-107 for the completion, which cites D-106.
14. **Roadmap order.** The roadmap lists its Progress lines oldest first, so Task 9 adds the S8.7 line below the S8.6 one.
15. **`check_plans.py` false positives.** The Global Constraints line that states the no-attribution rule is flagged as a possible attribution; it states the rule. The `make_icons.py` docstring line that names the Pillow version of the icons is flagged, as in S4.1 to S8.6.
